```
      attestationRef: args.inspectionSufficient ? "att:1" : null,
      attestedVersion:
        args.attestedVersion !== undefined
          ? args.attestedVersion
          : args.inspectionSufficient
            ? version
            : null,
      staleAttestationRef: null,
      reinspectionRequired: args.reinspectionRequired ?? !args.inspectionSufficient,
      reason: (args.inspectionSufficient
        ? "inspected"
        : "no_attestation") as "inspected" | "no_attestation",
      grantsAuthority: false as const,
    },
  };
}

describe("P6 R02 — action availability / currentness", () => {
  it("UI01 — EC id alone → no Confirm/Execute/Inspect", () => {
    const view = presentPilotExecution({
      continuityProjection: { ok: true, projection: baseProjection() },
      preExecutionContinuity: null,
    });
    expect(view.cta.kind).toBe("none");
  });

  it("UI02 — pre continuity failure → no premature action", () => {
    const view = presentPilotExecution({
      continuityProjection: { ok: true, projection: baseProjection() },
      preExecutionContinuity: {
        ok: false,
        code: "MATERIALITY_CURRENTNESS_STALE",
        message: "stale",
      },
    });
    expect(view.cta.kind).toBe("none");
    expect(view.attentionNote).toMatch(/refusée|MATERIALITY_CURRENTNESS_STALE/);
  });

  it("UI03 — kind=none → no premature action", () => {
    const view = presentPilotExecution({
      continuityProjection: { ok: true, projection: baseProjection() },
      preExecutionContinuity: { ok: true, kind: "none" },
    });
    expect(view.cta.kind).toBe("none");
  });

  it("UI04 — active + inspection insufficient → Inspect, not Confirm enabled", () => {
    const view = presentPilotExecution({
      continuityProjection: { ok: true, projection: baseProjection() },
      preExecutionContinuity: activePre({
        status: "confirmation_required",
        inspectionSufficient: false,
      }),
    });
    expect(view.cta.kind).toBe("inspect");
    expect(view.cta.kind === "inspect" && view.cta.enabled).toBe(true);
  });

  it("UI05 — active + inspection sufficient → Confirm enabled", () => {
    const view = presentPilotExecution({
      continuityProjection: { ok: true, projection: baseProjection() },
      preExecutionContinuity: activePre({
        status: "confirmation_required",
        inspectionSufficient: true,
      }),
    });
    expect(view.cta.kind).toBe("confirm");
    expect(view.cta.kind === "confirm" && view.cta.enabled).toBe(true);
  });

  it("UI06 — fingerprint drift → reinspect, Confirm not enabled", () => {
    const view = presentPilotExecution({
      continuityProjection: { ok: true, projection: baseProjection() },
      preExecutionContinuity: activePre({
        status: "confirmation_required",
        inspectionSufficient: true,
        fingerprint: "fp-new",
        inspectionFingerprint: "fp-old",
        reinspectionRequired: true,
      }),
    });
    expect(view.cta.kind).toBe("inspect");
    expect(view.attentionNote).toMatch(/empreinte|réinspection/i);
  });

  it("UI09 — confirmed ≠ authorized execute without inspection", () => {
    const view = presentPilotExecution({
      continuityProjection: {
        ok: true,
        projection: baseProjection({
          executionContractStatus: "confirmed",
        }),
      },
      preExecutionContinuity: activePre({
        status: "confirmed",
        inspectionSufficient: false,
      }),
    });
    expect(view.cta.kind).toBe("inspect");
    expect(view.cta.kind === "execute").toBe(false);
  });

  it("UI10 — terminal stage → no Confirm/Execute", () => {
    const view = presentPilotExecution({
      continuityProjection: {
        ok: true,
        projection: baseProjection({
          stage: "POST_EVIDENCE_COMPLETE",
          attemptId: "att:1",
          attemptStatus: "succeeded",
          evidenceId: "ev:1",
          postEvidencePresent: true,
          productOutcome: "PASS",
        }),
      },
      preExecutionContinuity: { ok: true, kind: "none" },
    });
    expect(view.status).toBe("terminee");
    expect(view.cta.kind).not.toBe("confirm");
    expect(view.cta.kind).not.toBe("execute");
    expect(view.cta.kind).not.toBe("inspect");
  });

  it("R3 C4/C7 — lifecycle unavailable never claims À jour", () => {
    const cur = presentCurrentness({
      transcriptAvailability: "available",
      stateVersion: 3,
      updatedAt: "2026-10-17T10:00:00.000Z",
      lifecycleProjectionAvailability: "unavailable",
    });
    expect(cur.label).not.toBe("À jour");
    expect(cur.tone).toBe("warn");
  });

  it("R3 C3 pending — not presented as À jour", () => {
    const cur = presentCurrentness({
      transcriptAvailability: "available",
      stateVersion: 3,
      lifecycleProjectionAvailability: "pending",
    });
    expect(cur.label).toBe("Lecture en cours");
  });
});
```

```
function deferred<T>() {
  let resolve!: (v: T) => void;
  let reject!: (e: unknown) => void;
  const promise = new Promise<T>((res, rej) => {
    resolve = res;
    reject = rej;
  });
  return { promise, resolve, reject };
}

function minimalLifecycleProjection(activeCycleInstanceId: string | null) {
  return {
    activeCycleInstanceId,
    selectedCycleInstanceId: activeCycleInstanceId,
    activeCycle: activeCycleInstanceId
      ? {
          cycleInstanceId: activeCycleInstanceId,
          createdAt: "2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z",
          status: "active",
        }
      : null,
    pausedCycles: [] as const,
    terminalCycles: [] as const,
    candidateCycles: [] as const,
    cycleReservations: [] as const,
    cycleWorkRecommendations: [] as const,
    cycleDecisions: [] as const,
    reservationSummary: { activeCount: 0 },
  };
}

function runtimeFor(projectId: string) {
  return {
    ok: true as const,
    project: {
      projectId,
      name: `Project ${projectId}`,
      shortReference: "P3",
      objective: "ctx freshness",
      contextSummary: "ctx",
      criticality: "STANDARD",
      constraints: [],
      localMode: true,
      source: "REAL_LOCAL_CORE",
      fixture: false,
      projectWorkspaceKey: null,
      repositoryBinding: null,
    },
    livingState: {
      id: `lps:${projectId}`,
      version: 3,
      createdAt: "2026-10-05T00:00:00.000Z",
      activeCycleInstanceId: null,
    },
    doctrine: { id: "d", version: "1", digest: "x", status: "RESOLVED" },
    readiness: {
      status: "NOT_READY",
      hard: "OPEN",
      tA6: "INCOMPLETE",
      iam: "NOT_SELECTED",
      productPersistence: "SQLITE_OA_PRODUCT_STORE",
      realAgentExecution: "DISABLED",
      delivery: "NOT_AUTHORIZED",
      cutover: "NOT_AUTHORIZED",
      runReady: false,
      productReady: false,
    },
    disclosures: {},
  };
}

describe("P6 R03 — context freshness async", () => {
  afterEach(() => {
    cleanup();
  });

  beforeEach(() => {
    vi.clearAllMocks();
    getProjectRuntimeActionMock.mockImplementation(async (id: string) =>
      runtimeFor(id),
    );
    useProductConversationMock.mockReturnValue({
      listRef: { current: null },
      messages: [],
      draft: "",
      setDraft: vi.fn(),
      toolEvents: [],
      busy: false,
      error: null,
      send: vi.fn(),
      stop: vi.fn(),
      transcriptAvailability: "available",
      journalEntries: [],
      activeProposal: null,
      decisionSubjectContinuity: null,
      governedExecutionContinuity: null,
      refreshConversationContinuity: vi.fn(),
      armReservationInteractionContext: vi.fn(),
      clearReservationResolutionProposal: vi.fn(),
      setActiveView: vi.fn(),
    });
  });

  it("CTX05 — pending load never shows À jour prematurely", async () => {
    const d = deferred<{ ok: true; projection: { activeCycleInstanceId: string } }>();
    lifecycleProjectionMock.mockReturnValue(d.promise);

    render(
      <ProjectWorkspacePage projectId="prj:ctx-a" onProjectName={vi.fn()} />,
    );

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toMatch(
        /Lecture en cours/,
      );
    });
    expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).not.toBe(
      "À jour",
    );

    await act(async () => {
      d.resolve({
        ok: true,
        projection: minimalLifecycleProjection("cyc:1") as never,
      });
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toBe(
        "À jour",
      );
    });
  });

  it("CTX01 — stale resolve for previous project is ignored", async () => {
    const first = deferred<{
      ok: true;
      projection: ReturnType<typeof minimalLifecycleProjection>;
    }>();
    const second = deferred<{
      ok: true;
      projection: ReturnType<typeof minimalLifecycleProjection>;
    }>();

    lifecycleProjectionMock.mockImplementation(
      ({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) => {
        if (projectId === "prj:ctx-old") return first.promise;
        return second.promise;
      },
    );

    const { rerender } = render(
      <ProjectWorkspacePage projectId="prj:ctx-old" onProjectName={vi.fn()} />,
    );

    rerender(
      <ProjectWorkspacePage projectId="prj:ctx-new" onProjectName={vi.fn()} />,
    );

    await act(async () => {
      second.resolve({
        ok: true,
        projection: minimalLifecycleProjection("cyc:new"),
      });
    });

    await act(async () => {
      first.resolve({
        ok: true,
        projection: minimalLifecycleProjection("cyc:old"),
      });
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(lifecycleProjectionMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({ projectId: "prj:ctx-new" }),
      );
    });
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toBe(
        "À jour",
      );
    });
  });

  it("CTX02/CTX06 — failed refresh marks unavailable (not À jour)", async () => {
    lifecycleProjectionMock.mockResolvedValue({
      ok: false,
      code: "UNAVAILABLE",
      message: "fail",
    });

    render(
      <ProjectWorkspacePage projectId="prj:ctx-fail" onProjectName={vi.fn()} />,
    );

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toMatch(
        /À vérifier/,
      );
    });
    expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).not.toBe(
      "À jour",
    );
  });

  it("CTX01 — distinguishing projects uses observable cycle ids, not only À jour badge", async () => {
    lifecycleProjectionMock.mockImplementation(
      ({ projectId }: { projectId: string }) =>
        Promise.resolve({
          ok: true as const,
          projection: minimalLifecycleProjection(
            projectId === "prj:ctx-a" ? "cyc:alpha" : "cyc:beta",
          ),
        }),
    );

    const { rerender } = render(
      <ProjectWorkspacePage projectId="prj:ctx-a" onProjectName={vi.fn()} />,
    );
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toBe(
        "À jour",
      );
    });
    expect(lifecycleProjectionMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.objectContaining({ projectId: "prj:ctx-a" }),
    );

    rerender(
      <ProjectWorkspacePage projectId="prj:ctx-b" onProjectName={vi.fn()} />,
    );
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(lifecycleProjectionMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
        expect.objectContaining({ projectId: "prj:ctx-b" }),
      );
    });
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toBe(
        "À jour",
      );
    });
    const calls = lifecycleProjectionMock.mock.calls.map(
      (c) => (c[0] as { projectId: string }).projectId,
    );
    expect(calls).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["prj:ctx-a", "prj:ctx-b"]));
  });

  it("CTX03 — concurrent lifecycle resolves do not leave a stuck pending chip", async () => {
    const a = deferred<{
      ok: true;
      projection: ReturnType<typeof minimalLifecycleProjection>;
    }>();
    const b = deferred<{
      ok: true;
      projection: ReturnType<typeof minimalLifecycleProjection>;
    }>();
    let n = 0;
    lifecycleProjectionMock.mockImplementation(() => {
      n += 1;
      return n === 1 ? a.promise : b.promise;
    });

    render(
      <ProjectWorkspacePage projectId="prj:ctx-race" onProjectName={vi.fn()} />,
    );
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toMatch(
        /Lecture en cours/,
      );
    });

    await act(async () => {
      b.resolve({
        ok: true,
        projection: minimalLifecycleProjection("cyc:second"),
      });
      a.resolve({
        ok: true,
        projection: minimalLifecycleProjection("cyc:first"),
      });
    });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toBe(
        "À jour",
      );
    });
  });

  it("CTX04/CTX08 — view navigation without Product mutation keeps currentness readable", async () => {
    lifecycleProjectionMock.mockResolvedValue({
      ok: true,
      projection: minimalLifecycleProjection("cyc:nav"),
    });
    render(
      <ProjectWorkspacePage projectId="prj:ctx-nav" onProjectName={vi.fn()} />,
    );
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toBe(
        "À jour",
      );
    });
    const before = lifecycleProjectionMock.mock.calls.length;
    // Switching tabs is presentation-only when no durable refresh is forced.
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("project-tab-execution"));
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByTestId("project-tab-conversation"));
    expect(lifecycleProjectionMock.mock.calls.length).toBe(before);
    expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toBe(
      "À jour",
    );
  });

  it("CTX07 — remount after failed lifecycle refresh recovers to À jour", async () => {
    lifecycleProjectionMock
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({
        ok: false,
        code: "UNAVAILABLE",
        message: "fail",
      })
      .mockResolvedValueOnce({
        ok: true,
        projection: minimalLifecycleProjection("cyc:retry"),
      });

    const { rerender } = render(
      <ProjectWorkspacePage projectId="prj:ctx-retry" onProjectName={vi.fn()} />,
    );
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toMatch(
        /À vérifier/,
      );
    });

    rerender(
      <ProjectWorkspacePage
        projectId="prj:ctx-retry"
        onProjectName={vi.fn()}
        key="retry-2"
      />,
    );
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId("project-currentness-chip").textContent).toBe(
        "À jour",
      );
    });
  });
});
```

```
/**
 * STUDIO-P6-EXECUTION-INSPECTION-CLOSURE-03
 * Disclosure → presented rows → attestable facts (ZERO REAL).
 */
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { ExecutionContractInspectionDisclosure } from "@/lib/oa/execution-contract";
import { buildInspectionFactRowsFromDisclosure } from "@/features/pre-m6-product-ui/surfaces/pilotExecutionPresentation";

function baseDisclosure(
  overrides: Partial<ExecutionContractInspectionDisclosure> = {},
): ExecutionContractInspectionDisclosure {
  return {
    action: "cursor.docs_write.apply",
    technicalTarget: "workspace.isolated.docs_write",
    scope: "studio.gcec.docs_write",
    targetRepositoryRef: "acme/demo",
    targetPath: "projects/demo/note.md",
    scopeIn: ["projects/demo"],
    scopeOut: null,
    createOrModify: true,
    noDelete: true,
    objective: "Note",
    artifactType: null,
    artifactBrief: null,
    contentRequirements: ["section A"],
    validationExpectations: null,
    expectedOutputs: ["projects/demo/note.md"],
    sourceGrounding: null,
    acceptanceCriteria: null,
    validationPlan: null,
    reportRequirements: null,
    evidenceRequirements: ["file_evidence"],
    requiredAuthority: "N2",
    requiredCapabilities: ["cursor.docs_write"],
    constraints: ["MATERIALITY:NON_STRUCTURAL"],
    stopConditions: ["STOP"],
    reversibility: "reversible",
    contractVersion: 1,
    executionContractId: "xct:1",
    semanticFingerprint: "fp-abc",
    disclosureComplete: true,
    incompletenessCode: null,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe("P6 R03 — inspection disclosure closure (presenter)", () => {
  it("INS01 — required facts with values are all rendered", () => {
    const required = [
      "action",
      "target",
      "scope",
      "requiredAuthority",
      "requiredCapabilities",
      "constraints",
      "stopConditions",
      "reversibility",
      "evidenceRequirements",
      "semanticFingerprint",
      "inputs.targetRepositoryRef",
      "inputs.targetPath",
      "inputs.scopeIn",
      "inputs.createOrModify",
      "inputs.noDelete",
      "inputs.contentRequirements",
      "expectedOutputs",
    ];
    const result = buildInspectionFactRowsFromDisclosure({
      disclosure: baseDisclosure(),
      requiredInspectedFacts: required,
    });
    expect(result.missingRequired).toEqual([]);
    expect(result.presentedFactIds).toEqual(required);
    expect(
      result.rows.find((r) => r.factId === "inputs.targetPath")?.value,
    ).toContain("note.md");
  });

  it("INS02 — missing required fact → not attestable", () => {
    const result = buildInspectionFactRowsFromDisclosure({
      disclosure: baseDisclosure({ targetPath: null }),
      requiredInspectedFacts: ["action", "inputs.targetPath"],
    });
    expect(result.missingRequired).toContain("inputs.targetPath");
    expect(result.presentedFactIds).not.toContain("inputs.targetPath");
  });

  it("INS04–INS07 — mission facts only when present in disclosure", () => {
    const withMission = buildInspectionFactRowsFromDisclosure({
      disclosure: baseDisclosure({
        sourceGrounding: {
          version: 1,
          honesty: "READ_GROUNDED",
          repositoryIdentity: "acme/demo",
          repositoryHeadSha: "abc123",
          cycleInstanceId: "cyc:1",
          declaredRepositorySources: ["projects/demo/a.md"],
          readRefs: [],
          unreadRequiredSources: [],
          searchIsNotRead: true,
          reconstructibleFrom: "durable_cycle_read_coverage",
        },
        acceptanceCriteria: [
          {
            criterionId: "c1",
            statement: "Artifact at path",
            kind: "artifact_at_path",
            expectedOutputRef: null,
            targetPath: "projects/demo/note.md",
          },
        ],
        validationPlan: ["run checks"],
        reportRequirements: ["summary"],
      }),
      requiredInspectedFacts: [
        "action",
        "inputs.sourceGrounding",
        "inputs.acceptanceCriteria",
        "inputs.validationPlan",
        "inputs.reportRequirements",
      ],
    });
    expect(withMission.missingRequired).toEqual([]);
    expect(withMission.presentedFactIds).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining([
        "inputs.sourceGrounding",
        "inputs.acceptanceCriteria",
        "inputs.validationPlan",
        "inputs.reportRequirements",
      ]),
    );

    const without = buildInspectionFactRowsFromDisclosure({
      disclosure: baseDisclosure(),
      requiredInspectedFacts: ["action", "inputs.sourceGrounding"],
    });
    expect(without.missingRequired).toContain("inputs.sourceGrounding");
  });

  it("INS20 — presented IDs equal rendered rows (no parallel invent)", () => {
    const required = ["action", "scope", "inputs.targetPath"];
    const result = buildInspectionFactRowsFromDisclosure({
      disclosure: baseDisclosure(),
      requiredInspectedFacts: required,
    });
    expect(result.rows.map((r) => r.factId)).toEqual(result.presentedFactIds);
    expect(result.presentedFactIds.every((id) => required.includes(id))).toBe(
      true,
    );
  });
});
```

```
describe("Pass04 unified inspection — shared helpers (U04/U06/U07)", () => {
  it("U04 — buildInspectionExamView uses only canonical required facts", () => {
    const view = buildInspectionExamView({
      disclosure: disclosure({
        sourceGrounding: { kind: "repo", refs: ["r1"] },
        acceptanceCriteria: [
          { criterionId: "c1", statement: "ok", kind: "must" },
        ],
        validationPlan: ["step-a"],
        reportRequirements: ["report-1"],
      }),
      requiredInspectedFacts: [
        ...REQUIRED,
        "inputs.sourceGrounding",
        "inputs.acceptanceCriteria",
        "inputs.validationPlan",
        "inputs.reportRequirements",
      ],
    });
    expect(view.disclosureIncomplete).toBe(false);
    expect(view.missingRequired).toEqual([]);
    expect(view.presentedFactIds).toEqual(
      expect.arrayContaining([
        "inputs.sourceGrounding",
        "inputs.acceptanceCriteria",
        "inputs.validationPlan",
        "inputs.reportRequirements",
      ]),
    );
  });

  it("U06 — missing required fact blocks attestation payload", () => {
    const view = buildInspectionExamView({
      disclosure: disclosure({ targetPath: null }),
      requiredInspectedFacts: [...REQUIRED],
    });
    expect(view.missingRequired).toContain("inputs.targetPath");
    expect(view.presentedFactIds).not.toContain("inputs.targetPath");
  });

  it("U10/U11/U12 — snapshot identity comparison", () => {
    const a = {
      projectId: "p1",
      executionContractId: "x1",
      version: 1,
      semanticFingerprint: "fp1",
    };
    expect(sameInspectionExamSnapshot(a, { ...a })).toBe(true);
    expect(sameInspectionExamSnapshot(a, { ...a, version: 2 })).toBe(false);
    expect(
      sameInspectionExamSnapshot(a, { ...a, semanticFingerprint: "fp2" }),
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(
      sameInspectionExamSnapshot(a, { ...a, executionContractId: "x2" }),
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(sameInspectionExamSnapshot(a, { ...a, projectId: "p2" })).toBe(
      false,
    );
  });
});
```

```
/**
 * MATERIALITY-BLOCK-01 — Generic PREPARE documentary destination bind.
 *
 * Pathless framing documentary mission + repositoryBinding + active Cadrage
 * → Product composes note-de-cadrage.md under pathRoot/01-cadrage before
 * materiality → inspectable EC. No HD. No Attempt. No Cursor REAL.
 *
 * @vitest-environment node
 */
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  buildIntentAnalysisFromSolicitedMission,
  prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft,
  rememberGenericPrepareRecommendation,
  analysisFromPendingGenericPrepareOffer,
  solicitGenericExecutionPrepare,
  getCurrentGenericPrepareOffer,
} from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft";
import {
  concretePathsAreOnlyBareMarkdownLeaves,
  isBareSafeMarkdownLeafPath,
  isGenericPrepareDocumentaryMission,
  resolveGenericPrepareDocumentaryTarget,
} from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/resolveGenericPrepareDocumentaryTarget";
import { resolveNonAuthoritativeDocumentaryArtifactLeafCandidate } from "@/lib/oa/project/domain/artifactTargetRouting";
import { collectConcreteMissionPaths } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/resolveAuthoritativeProductMateriality";
import { resetPendingGenericPrepareOfferStoreForTests } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/pendingGenericPrepareOfferStore";
import {
  LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
  registerLocalPiloteAuthority,
} from "@/lib/oa/decision";
import { ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton } from "@/lib/oa/project/infrastructure/managedRepoPathFacts";
import { SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime/managedRepoRootBaseConfig";
import {
  bootW2Runtime,
  cleanupW2TempDirs,
  tempProductDbPath,
  W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
} from "./w2Harness";
import type { RuntimeApplicationService } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime";

function restoreEnvVar(name: string, previous: string | undefined): void {
  if (previous === undefined) delete process.env[name];
  else process.env[name] = previous;
}

describe("MATERIALITY-BLOCK-01 documentary Generic PREPARE bind", () => {
  let runtime: RuntimeApplicationService;
  let previousMorris: string | undefined;
  let previousDetPin: string | undefined;
  let sessionDbPath: string;

  beforeEach(() => {
    previousMorris = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY;
    previousDetPin = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY = "1";
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA = W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_CURSOR_REAL = "0";
    resetPendingGenericPrepareOfferStoreForTests();
    sessionDbPath = tempProductDbPath("mat-block01-session.sqlite");
    runtime = bootW2Runtime({
      productDbPath: tempProductDbPath("mat-block01-product.sqlite"),
      idPrefix: "mb01",
    });
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    resetPendingGenericPrepareOfferStoreForTests();
    cleanupW2TempDirs();
    restoreEnvVar("SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY", previousMorris);
    restoreEnvVar("SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA", previousDetPin);
  });

  async function seedFramingProject(suffix: string) {
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const created = await runtime.createProject({
      name: `MB01 ${suffix}`,
      objective: "Organisation des bénévoles (fixture)",
      context: "mat-block-01",
      criticality: "STANDARD",
      constraints: ["ZERO REAL"],
      shortReference: `MB${suffix.toUpperCase().slice(0, 4)}`,
      idempotencyKey: `idem:mb01-${suffix}`,
    });
    if (!created.ok) throw new Error("createProject");
    const projectId = created.project.projectId;
    const pathRoot = `projects/mb01-${suffix}`;

    const lps0 = await oa.projectServices.getCurrentLivingProjectState.execute({
      projectId,
    });
    if (!lps0.ok) throw new Error("lps0");
    await oa.cycleServices.createInitialTrajectory.execute({
      trajectoryId: `trj:${projectId}`,
      projectId,
      steps: [{ stepId: "stp:a", order: 1, label: "A", state: "done" }],
      status: "active",
      expectedLpsVersion: lps0.livingProjectState.version,
      createdBy: {
        actorId: "actor:morris",
        role: "project_owner",
        displayName: "Morris",
        authorityLevel: "N3",
      },
    });

    const cycleInstanceId = `cyc:mb01-${suffix}`;
    await oa.cycleServices.createCycle.execute({
      cycleInstanceId,
      cycleTypeId: "cyc:framing",
      projectId,
      signals: { lowRiskBounded: true },
      createdBy: {
        actorId: "actor:nora-f2",
        role: "agent",
        displayName: "Nora F2",
        authorityLevel: "N1",
      },
      linkAsActiveCycle: false,
    });
    const auth = registerLocalPiloteAuthority({
      authorityResolver: oa.authorityResolver,
      scope: `pilot-lifecycle:${cycleInstanceId}`,
      issuedAt: "2026-10-17T10:00:00.000Z",
      forceEnable: true,
    });
    if (!auth.ok) throw new Error("auth");
    const lps1 = await oa.projectServices.getCurrentLivingProjectState.execute({
      projectId,
    });
    if (!lps1.ok) throw new Error("lps1");
    const started = await oa.cycleServices.pilotLifecycle.start({
      cycleInstanceId,
      projectId,
      createdBy: {
        actorId: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.actorId,
        role: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.role,
        displayName: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.displayName,
        authorityLevel: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.authorityLevel,
      },
      authorityEvidenceId: auth.evidenceId,
      expectedLpsVersion: lps1.livingProjectState.version,
    });
    if (!started.ok) throw new Error(`start: ${started.error?.detailCode}`);

    const identity = `acme/mb01-${suffix}`;
    await oa.projectServices.setProjectRepositoryBinding!.execute({
      projectId,
      actor: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
      binding: {
        provider: "github",
        identity,
        remoteUrl: `https://github.com/${identity}.git`,
        defaultBranch: "main",
        pathRoot,
      },
    });
    ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton({
      managedRepoRootBase: process.env[SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV]!,
      identity,
    });
    return { projectId, cycleInstanceId, oa, pathRoot, identity };
  }

  const pathlessFramingMission = {
    objective:
      "Réaliser un document de cadrage du projet dans le dépôt Git, en consolidant besoins, objectifs et points ouverts.",
    expectedOutcome:
      "Un document de cadrage lisible et réutilisable, ajouté dans un emplacement documentaire approprié du dépôt.",
    scope:
      "Documentation du projet dans le dépôt Git ; chemin précis à déterminer après inspection. Exclure code et architecture.",
    contextNotes:
      "Le projet est en Cadrage actif. Version de travail à examiner avant validation. Ne pas clore le cycle.",
  };

  it("leaf convention — framing note/document de cadrage → note-de-cadrage.md", () => {
    expect(
      resolveNonAuthoritativeDocumentaryArtifactLeafCandidate({
        textFields: ["Réaliser un document de cadrage dans le dépôt"],
        cycleTypeId: "cyc:framing",
      }),
    ).toBe("note-de-cadrage.md");
    expect(
      resolveNonAuthoritativeDocumentaryArtifactLeafCandidate({
        textFields: ["Refactorer le module d'export"],
        cycleTypeId: "cyc:framing",
      }),
    ).toBeNull();
  });

  it("A — pathless framing documentary solicit → EC with Product target under 01-cadrage", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId, oa, pathRoot } =
      await seedFramingProject("a");
    const expectedTarget = `${pathRoot}/01-cadrage/note-de-cadrage.md`;
    const expectedCycleRoot = `${pathRoot}/01-cadrage`;

    const analysis = buildIntentAnalysisFromSolicitedMission(
      pathlessFramingMission,
    );
    expect(analysis.executionIntent).toBeNull();
    expect(collectConcreteMissionPaths(analysis)).toEqual([]);
    expect(isGenericPrepareDocumentaryMission(analysis)).toBe(true);

    const bind = await resolveGenericPrepareDocumentaryTarget({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId,
      analysis,
    });
    expect(bind.kind).toBe("resolved");
    if (bind.kind !== "resolved") throw new Error("expected resolved");
    expect(bind.resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath).toBe(expectedTarget);
    expect(bind.artifactFileName).toBe("note-de-cadrage.md");
    expect(bind.cycleWorkspaceRoot).toBe(expectedCycleRoot);

    const prep = await solicitGenericExecutionPrepare({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId,

      sessionDbPath,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,

    expect(prep.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(prep.message);
    expect(prep.code).toBe("GENERIC_PREPARE_DRAFTED");
    expect(prep.humanDecisionCreated).toBe(false);
    expect(prep.executionAttemptCreated).toBe(false);
    expect(prep.materiality).not.toBe("unknown");

    const loaded = await oa.executionContractServices.getExecutionContract.execute({
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,

    expect(loaded.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!loaded.ok) throw new Error("load");
    expect(loaded.contract.decisionRefs ?? []).toEqual([]);
    expect(loaded.contract.inputs?.documentaryTargetResolution).toBe(

    );

    expect(loaded.contract.inputs?.targetPath).toBe(expectedTarget);
    expect(loaded.contract.inputs?.proposedTargetPathAuthoritative).toBe(false);
    expect(loaded.contract.inputs?.scopeIn).toEqual([expectedCycleRoot]);
    expect(loaded.contract.expectedOutputs).toContain(expectedTarget);
    expect(prep.executionAttemptCreated).toBe(false);
  });

    const offer = getCurrentGenericPrepareOffer(projectId, { sessionDbPath });

    // Offer may still lack concrete paths — Product resolves at PREPARE time.

    const fromOffer = analysisFromPendingGenericPrepareOffer(offer!);

    expect(missingProject.kind).toBe("blocked");

    expect(bind.code).toBe("DOCUMENTARY_TARGET_FILENAME_REQUIRED");

    expect(prep.contract.inputs?.scopeIn).toEqual([expectedCycleRoot]);
    expect(prep.humanDecisionCreated).toBe(false);
```

```
/**
 * P6 — G-D4 Select inspection binding (R1).
 *
 * Proves stamped Generic Select never overwrites an explicit binding half,
 * refuses partial/contradictory pairs, and resolves from durable attestation
 * only when BOTH version and fingerprint are absent.
 *
 * Fake Cursor boundary only — ZERO Cursor REAL / OpenAI LIVE.
 *
 * @vitest-environment node
 */
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import path from "node:path";
import { governedExecuteSelectAgent } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/governedExecuteAuthorizedContract";
import { solicitGenericExecutionPrepare } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft";
import { inspectExecutionContract } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/inspectExecutionContract";
import { authorizeGenericPreparedContract } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/authorizeGenericPreparedContract";
import { amendExecutionContractWithConstraint } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/amendExecutionContract";
import { confirmExecutionContractForAuthorization } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/confirmForAuthorization";
import { LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR } from "@/lib/oa/decision";
import { STUDIO_CURSOR_GENERALIST_AGENT_ID } from "@/lib/oa/execution-attempt";
import { ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton } from "@/lib/oa/project/infrastructure/managedRepoPathFacts";
import { SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime/managedRepoRootBaseConfig";
import {
  bootW2Runtime,
  cleanupW2TempDirs,
  tempProductDbPath,
  W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
} from "./w2Harness";
import type { RuntimeApplicationService } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime";

const APP = path.resolve(__dirname, "../..");

describe("P6 Select inspection binding (R1)", () => {
  let runtime: RuntimeApplicationService;
  let sessionDbPath: string;
  let previousMorris: string | undefined;
  let previousDetPin: string | undefined;

  beforeEach(() => {
    previousMorris = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY;
    previousDetPin = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY = "1";
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA = W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    sessionDbPath = tempProductDbPath("p6-ibind.sqlite");
    runtime = bootW2Runtime({
      productDbPath: sessionDbPath,
      idPrefix: "ibind",
    });
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    cleanupW2TempDirs();
    if (previousMorris === undefined) {
      delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY;
    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY = previousMorris;
    }
    if (previousDetPin === undefined) {
      delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA = previousDetPin;
    }
  });

  async function seedProject(suffix: string) {
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const created = await runtime.createProject({
      name: `IBIND ${suffix}`,
      objective: "Inspection binding Select",
      context: "det ibind",
      criticality: "STANDARD",
      constraints: ["ZERO REAL"],
      shortReference: `IB${suffix.toUpperCase().slice(0, 6)}`,
      idempotencyKey: `idem:ibind-${suffix}`,
    });
    if (!created.ok) throw new Error("createProject");
    const projectId = created.project.projectId;
    const lps0 = await oa.projectServices.getCurrentLivingProjectState.execute({
      projectId,
    });
    if (!lps0.ok) throw new Error("lps0");
    await oa.cycleServices.createInitialTrajectory.execute({
      trajectoryId: `trj:${projectId}`,
      projectId,
      steps: [{ stepId: "stp:a", order: 1, label: "A", state: "done" }],
      status: "active",
      expectedLpsVersion: lps0.livingProjectState.version,
      createdBy: {
        actorId: "actor:morris",
        role: "project_owner",
        displayName: "Morris",
        authorityLevel: "N3",
      },
    });
    const identity = `acme/ibind-${suffix}`;
    await oa.projectServices.setProjectRepositoryBinding!.execute({
      projectId,
      actor: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
      binding: {
        provider: "github",
        identity,
        remoteUrl: `https://github.com/${identity}.git`,
        defaultBranch: "main",
        pathRoot: "projects/demo",
      },
    });
    ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton({
      managedRepoRootBase: process.env[SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV]!,
      identity,
    });
    return { projectId, oa };
  }

  async function prepareInspectAuthorize(suffix: string) {
    const { projectId, oa } = await seedProject(suffix);
    const prep = await solicitGenericExecutionPrepare({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
      mission: {
        objective: `Mission binding ${suffix}`,
        expectedOutcome: "Résultat",
        scope: "projects/demo/src",
      },
      sessionDbPath,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
    });
    if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(`prep ${prep.code}`);
    const inspected = await inspectExecutionContract({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
    });
    if (!inspected.ok) throw new Error(`inspect ${inspected.message}`);
    const authorized = await authorizeGenericPreparedContract({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      expectedVersion: inspected.contractVersion,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: inspected.semanticFingerprint,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    if (!authorized.ok) throw new Error(`auth ${authorized.code}`);
    return { projectId, oa, prep, inspected, authorized };
  }

  async function countAttempts(
    oa: NonNullable<RuntimeApplicationService["oa"]>,
    projectId: string,
  ): Promise<number> {
    const contracts = await oa.executionContractServices.contracts.listByProject(
      projectId,
    );
    let total = 0;
    for (const c of contracts) {
      total += (
        await oa.executionAttemptServices!.attempts.listByContract(
          c.executionContractId,
        )
      ).length;
    }
    return total;
  }

  it("static — partial binding no longer triggers attestation overwrite of the other half", () => {
    const src = readFileSync(
      path.join(
        APP,
        "features/project-assistant/w2/governedExecuteAuthorizedContract.ts",
      ),
      "utf8",
    );
    expect(src).toMatch(/BINDING_INCOMPLETE/);
    expect(src).toMatch(/resolveStampedSelectInspectionBinding/);
    expect(src).toMatch(/caller_explicit/);
    expect(src).toMatch(/durable_attestation/);
    // Old OR-gate that overwrote both when either half missing must be gone.
    expect(src).not.toMatch(
      /typeof expectedVersion !== "number"[\s\S]*\|\|[\s\S]*!expectedSemanticFingerprint\.trim\(\)/,
    );
  });

  it("I1 — no params + valid durable attestation → Select accepted", async () => {
    const ctx = await prepareInspectAuthorize("i1");
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: ctx.prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!selected.ok) throw new Error(selected.message);
    expect(selected.selectedAgentRef).toBe(STUDIO_CURSOR_GENERALIST_AGENT_ID);
  });

  it("I2 — both explicit valid params → Select accepted (no overwrite path)", async () => {
    const ctx = await prepareInspectAuthorize("i2");
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: ctx.prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      expectedVersion: ctx.inspected.contractVersion,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: ctx.inspected.semanticFingerprint,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!selected.ok) throw new Error(selected.message);
    expect(selected.selectedAgentRef).toBe(STUDIO_CURSOR_GENERALIST_AGENT_ID);
  });

  it("I3 — version only → BINDING_INCOMPLETE, no Attempt", async () => {
    const ctx = await prepareInspectAuthorize("i3");
    const before = await countAttempts(ctx.oa, ctx.projectId);
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: ctx.prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      expectedVersion: ctx.inspected.contractVersion,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("BINDING_INCOMPLETE");
    expect(await countAttempts(ctx.oa, ctx.projectId)).toBe(before);
  });

  it("I4 — fingerprint only → BINDING_INCOMPLETE, no Attempt", async () => {
    const ctx = await prepareInspectAuthorize("i4");
    const before = await countAttempts(ctx.oa, ctx.projectId);
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: ctx.prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: ctx.inspected.semanticFingerprint,
    });

    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("BINDING_INCOMPLETE");
    expect(await countAttempts(ctx.oa, ctx.projectId)).toBe(before);
  });

  it("I5 — false version + fingerprint absent → BINDING_INCOMPLETE (no attestation overwrite)", async () => {
    const ctx = await prepareInspectAuthorize("i5");

    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: ctx.prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      expectedVersion: 999_999,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("BINDING_INCOMPLETE");
    expect(await countAttempts(ctx.oa, ctx.projectId)).toBe(before);
  });

      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: "sha256:deadbeef".padEnd(71, "0"),
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("BINDING_INCOMPLETE");
  });

  it("I7 — contradictory explicit pair → refuse (mismatch), no Attempt", async () => {
    const ctx = await prepareInspectAuthorize("i7");
    const before = await countAttempts(ctx.oa, ctx.projectId);
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: ctx.prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      expectedVersion: ctx.inspected.contractVersion,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: "sha256:deadbeef".padEnd(71, "0"),
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(
      selected.code === "FINGERPRINT_MISMATCH" ||
        selected.code === "VERSION_MISMATCH" ||
        selected.code === "NOT_AUTHORIZED",
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(await countAttempts(ctx.oa, ctx.projectId)).toBe(before);
  });

  it("I8 — attestation absent (no inspect) → INSPECTION_REQUIRED, no Attempt", async () => {
    const { projectId, oa } = await seedProject("i8");
    const prep = await solicitGenericExecutionPrepare({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
      mission: {
        objective: "Mission sans inspection",
        expectedOutcome: "n/a",
        scope: "projects/demo/src",
      },
      sessionDbPath,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
    });
    if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(prep.message);
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(
      selected.code === "INSPECTION_REQUIRED" ||
        selected.code === "EXPECTED_VERSION_REQUIRED" ||
        selected.code === "NOT_AUTHORIZED",
    ).toBe(true);
    expect(await countAttempts(oa, projectId)).toBe(0);
  });

  it("I9/I10 — amended contract → reinspection / binding refuse, no Attempt", async () => {
    const ctx = await prepareInspectAuthorize("i10");
    const amended = await amendExecutionContractWithConstraint({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: ctx.prep.executionContractId,
      additionalConstraint: "AMEND_TEST_BINDING_STALE",
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(amended.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!amended.ok) throw new Error(amended.message);
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: amended.successor.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      expectedVersion: ctx.inspected.contractVersion,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: ctx.inspected.semanticFingerprint,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(await countAttempts(ctx.oa, ctx.projectId)).toBe(0);
  });

  it("I11 — Confirm may bump version; attested binding still Select-valid via I1 path", async () => {
    const { projectId, oa } = await seedProject("i11");
    const prep = await solicitGenericExecutionPrepare({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
      mission: {
        objective: "Mission confirm binding",
        expectedOutcome: "n/a",
        scope: "projects/demo/src",
      },
      sessionDbPath,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
    });
    if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(prep.message);
    const inspected = await inspectExecutionContract({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
    });
    if (!inspected.ok) throw new Error(inspected.message);
    // Confirm when required (status confirmation_required) then authorize.
    const live = await oa.executionContractServices.getExecutionContract.execute({
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
    });
    if (!live.ok) throw new Error("get");
    if (live.contract.status === "confirmation_required") {
      const confirmed = await confirmExecutionContractForAuthorization({
        oa,
        projectId,
        executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
        forceLocalAuthority: true,
      });
      expect(confirmed.ok).toBe(true);
    }
    const authorized = await authorizeGenericPreparedContract({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      expectedVersion: inspected.contractVersion,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: inspected.semanticFingerprint,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    // Authorize binds to inspected version/fp (attested survives Confirm).
    expect(authorized.ok).toBe(true);
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!selected.ok) throw new Error(selected.message);
  });

  it("I12/I13 — refused partial binding creates neither Attempt nor HumanDecision", async () => {
    const ctx = await prepareInspectAuthorize("i12");
    const hdBefore = (
      await ctx.oa.decisionServices.decisions.listByProject(ctx.projectId)
    ).length;
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: ctx.prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      expectedVersion: ctx.inspected.contractVersion,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("BINDING_INCOMPLETE");
    expect(await countAttempts(ctx.oa, ctx.projectId)).toBe(0);
    expect(
      (await ctx.oa.decisionServices.decisions.listByProject(ctx.projectId))
        .length,
    ).toBe(hdBefore);
  });

  it("I5b — explicit NaN version (with fingerprint) → BINDING_INVALID, not attestation", async () => {
    const ctx = await prepareInspectAuthorize("i5b");
    const before = await countAttempts(ctx.oa, ctx.projectId);
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: ctx.prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      expectedVersion: Number.NaN,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: ctx.inspected.semanticFingerprint,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("BINDING_INVALID");
    expect(await countAttempts(ctx.oa, ctx.projectId)).toBe(before);
  });

  it("I6b — explicit whitespace fingerprint (with version) → BINDING_INVALID", async () => {
    const ctx = await prepareInspectAuthorize("i6b");
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: ctx.prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      expectedVersion: ctx.inspected.contractVersion,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: "   ",
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("BINDING_INVALID");
  });

  it("I8b — malformed fingerprint alone → BINDING_INVALID (not treated as absent)", async () => {
    const ctx = await prepareInspectAuthorize("i8b");
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: ctx.oa,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      executionContractId: ctx.prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: "not-a-valid-fingerprint",
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("BINDING_INVALID");
  });
});
```

```
/**
 * P6 — M-HD-01 Final EC/HD Authority Binding Correction.
 * IC: EC internal dual-representation consistency.
 * CM: CURRENT Product materiality continuity.
 *
 * Fake only — ZERO Cursor REAL / OpenAI LIVE / Pilot UI claim.
 *
 * @vitest-environment node
 */
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  buildIntentAnalysisFromSolicitedMission,
  prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft,
} from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft";
import {
  recordGenericMissionHumanDecision,
  sealGenericMissionForPresentation,
} from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/recordGenericMissionHumanDecision";
import {
  GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_ACCEPT_PREPARE,
  assertExecutionContractInternalConsistency,
  materialityContinuityCompatible,
} from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/genericMissionSubject";
import { verifyGenericMissionHumanDecisionForContract } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/verifyGenericMissionHumanDecisionForPrepare";
import { inspectExecutionContract } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/inspectExecutionContract";
import { authorizeGenericPreparedContract } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/authorizeGenericPreparedContract";
import { confirmExecutionContractForAuthorization } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/confirmForAuthorization";
import { LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR } from "@/lib/oa/decision";
import type { ExecutionContract } from "@/lib/oa/execution-contract";
import { ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton } from "@/lib/oa/project/infrastructure/managedRepoPathFacts";
import { SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime/managedRepoRootBaseConfig";
import {
  bootW2Runtime,
  cleanupW2TempDirs,
  tempProductDbPath,
  W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
} from "./w2Harness";
import type { RuntimeApplicationService } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime";
import type { IntentAnalysisDto } from "@/features/project-assistant/f2/types";

function structuralAnalysis(overrides?: {
  objective?: string;
  scope?: string;
}): IntentAnalysisDto {
  const scope = overrides?.scope ?? "projects/demo/src/final-bind";
  const base = buildIntentAnalysisFromSolicitedMission({
    objective: overrides?.objective ?? "Mission structurante final binding",
    expectedOutcome: "Autorité EC/HD cohérente",
    scope,
    impactSignals: {
      structuralChange: true,
      architectureImpact: true,
    },
  });
  return {
    ...base,
    stopConditions: ["STOP_FINAL_A"],
    risks: ["RISK_FINAL"],
    reservations: ["RES_FINAL"],
    executionIntent: {
      intentKind: "code_change",
      artifactType: "module",
      scopeIn: [scope],
      requiredCapabilities: [],
      requestedOperation: "final-bind",
      reversibilityExpectation: "reversible",
    },
  };
}

function cloneMutated(
  contract: ExecutionContract,
  mutate: (c: ExecutionContract) => void,
): ExecutionContract {
  const copy = structuredClone(contract) as ExecutionContract;
  mutate(copy);
  return copy;
}

describe("P6 M-HD-01 Final Authority Binding (IC + CM)", () => {
  let runtime: RuntimeApplicationService;
  let sessionDbPath: string;
  let previousMorris: string | undefined;
  let previousPilot: string | undefined;
  let previousDetPin: string | undefined;

  beforeEach(() => {
    previousMorris = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY;
    previousPilot = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_LOCAL_PILOT_AUTHORITY;
    previousDetPin = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY = "1";
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_LOCAL_PILOT_AUTHORITY = "1";
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA = W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    sessionDbPath = tempProductDbPath("p6-mhd01-final.sqlite");
    runtime = bootW2Runtime({
      productDbPath: sessionDbPath,
      idPrefix: "mhdfin",
    });
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    cleanupW2TempDirs();
    if (previousMorris === undefined) {
      delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY;
    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY = previousMorris;
    }
    if (previousPilot === undefined) {
      delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_LOCAL_PILOT_AUTHORITY;
    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_LOCAL_PILOT_AUTHORITY = previousPilot;
    }
    if (previousDetPin === undefined) {
      delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA = previousDetPin;
    }
  });

  async function seedProject(suffix: string) {
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const created = await runtime.createProject({
      name: `MHDFIN ${suffix}`,
      objective: "M-HD-01 final authority binding",
      context: "det final",
      criticality: "STANDARD",
      constraints: ["ZERO REAL"],
      shortReference: `MF${suffix.toUpperCase().slice(0, 6)}`,
      idempotencyKey: `idem:mhdfin-${suffix}`,
    });
    if (!created.ok) throw new Error("createProject");
    const projectId = created.project.projectId;
    const lps0 = await oa.projectServices.getCurrentLivingProjectState.execute({
      projectId,
    });
    if (!lps0.ok) throw new Error("lps0");
    await oa.cycleServices.createInitialTrajectory.execute({
      trajectoryId: `trj:${projectId}`,
      projectId,
      steps: [{ stepId: "stp:a", order: 1, label: "A", state: "done" }],
      status: "active",
      expectedLpsVersion: lps0.livingProjectState.version,
      createdBy: {
        actorId: "actor:morris",
        role: "project_owner",
        displayName: "Morris",
        authorityLevel: "N3",
      },
    });
    const identity = `acme/mhdfin-${suffix}`;
    await oa.projectServices.setProjectRepositoryBinding!.execute({
      projectId,
      actor: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
      binding: {
        provider: "github",
        identity,
        remoteUrl: `https://github.com/${identity}.git`,
        defaultBranch: "main",
        pathRoot: "projects/demo",
      },
    });
    ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton({
      managedRepoRootBase: process.env[SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV]!,
      identity,
    });
    return { projectId, oa, identity };
  }

  async function prepareStructuralWithHd(suffix: string) {
    const { projectId, oa, identity } = await seedProject(suffix);
    const analysis = structuralAnalysis();
    const sealed = await sealGenericMissionForPresentation({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
      analysis,
    });
    if (!sealed.ok) throw new Error(sealed.message);
    const recorded = await recordGenericMissionHumanDecision({
      oa,
      subject: sealed.subject,
      selectedOptionId: GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_ACCEPT_PREPARE,
      analysis,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    if (!recorded.ok) throw new Error(recorded.message);
    const prep = await prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
      analysis,
      decisionId: recorded.decision.decisionId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
    });
    if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(`${prep.code} ${prep.message}`);
    const inspected = await inspectExecutionContract({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
    });
    if (!inspected.ok) throw new Error(inspected.message);
    const live = await oa.executionContractServices.getExecutionContract.execute({
      executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
    });
    if (!live.ok) throw new Error("get EC");
    return {
      projectId,
      oa,
      identity,
      analysis,
      decision: recorded.decision,
      subject: sealed.subject,
      prep,
      inspected,
      contract: live.contract,
    };
  }

  describe("IC — EC internal consistency", () => {
    it("IC01 — EC cohérent → Confirm/Authorize PASS", async () => {
      const ctx = await prepareStructuralWithHd("ic01");
      const internal = assertExecutionContractInternalConsistency({
        constraints: ctx.contract.constraints,
        inputs: ctx.contract.inputs as Record<string, unknown>,
      });
      expect(internal.ok).toBe(true);
      if (ctx.contract.status === "confirmation_required") {
        const confirmed = await confirmExecutionContractForAuthorization({
          oa: ctx.oa,
          projectId: ctx.projectId,
          executionContractId: ctx.contract.executionContractId,
          forceLocalAuthority: true,
        });
        expect(confirmed.ok).toBe(true);
      }
      const authorized = await authorizeGenericPreparedContract({
        oa: ctx.oa,
        projectId: ctx.projectId,
        executionContractId: ctx.contract.executionContractId,
        expectedVersion: ctx.inspected.contractVersion,
        expectedSemanticFingerprint: ctx.inspected.semanticFingerprint,
        forceLocalAuthority: true,
      });
      expect(authorized.ok).toBe(true);
    });

    it("IC02 — facts STRUCTURAL vs constraint NON_STRUCTURAL → REFUS", async () => {
      const ctx = await prepareStructuralWithHd("ic02");
      const mutated = cloneMutated(ctx.contract, (c) => {
        c.constraints = (c.constraints ?? [])
          .filter((x) => !x.startsWith("MATERIALITY:"))
          .concat(["MATERIALITY:NON_STRUCTURAL"]);
        c.inputs = {
          ...c.inputs,
          materialityFacts: {
            ...((c.inputs?.materialityFacts as object) ?? {}),
            assessedMateriality: "structural",
          },
        };
      });
      const v = await verifyGenericMissionHumanDecisionForContract({
        oa: ctx.oa,
        projectId: ctx.projectId,
        decisionId: ctx.decision.decisionId,
        contract: mutated,
      });
      expect(v.ok).toBe(false);
      if (v.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
      expect(v.message).toContain("materiality_facts_vs_constraint_conflict");
    });

    it("IC03 — facts NON_STRUCTURAL vs constraint STRUCTURAL → REFUS", async () => {
      const ctx = await prepareStructuralWithHd("ic03");
      const mutated = cloneMutated(ctx.contract, (c) => {
        c.constraints = (c.constraints ?? [])
          .filter((x) => !x.startsWith("MATERIALITY:"))
          .concat(["MATERIALITY:STRUCTURAL"]);
        c.inputs = {
          ...c.inputs,
          materialityFacts: {
            ...((c.inputs?.materialityFacts as object) ?? {}),
            assessedMateriality: "non_structural",
          },
        };
      });
      const internal = assertExecutionContractInternalConsistency({
        constraints: mutated.constraints,
        inputs: mutated.inputs as Record<string, unknown>,
      });
      expect(internal.ok).toBe(false);
      if (internal.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
      expect(internal.reason).toBe("materiality_facts_vs_constraint_conflict");
    });

    it("IC04 — deux tokens MATERIALITY contradictoires → REFUS", () => {

    it("IC09 — assessedMateriality invalide ne devient pas absence", () => {
      const internal = assertExecutionContractInternalConsistency({
        constraints: ["MATERIALITY:STRUCTURAL"],

          missionSubjectId: "gms:x",
          presentationDigest: "abc",
          materialityFacts: { assessedMateriality: "not-a-class" },
        },
      });
      expect(internal.ok).toBe(false);
      if (internal.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
      expect(internal.reason).toBe("materiality_facts_malformed");
    });

    it("IC10 — contrat historique sans stamp generic_mission → legacy OK", () => {
      const internal = assertExecutionContractInternalConsistency({
        constraints: ["MATERIALITY:NON_STRUCTURAL", "PRODUCT_GOVERNED"],
        inputs: {
          objective: "legacy",
          // no missionSubjectId / presentationDigest
        },
      });
      expect(internal.ok).toBe(true);
    });
  });

  describe("CM — Product current materiality continuity", () => {
    it("CM01 — structural+HD + Product concordant → Confirm/Authorize PASS", async () => {
      const ctx = await prepareStructuralWithHd("cm01");
      if (ctx.contract.status === "confirmation_required") {
        const confirmed = await confirmExecutionContractForAuthorization({
          oa: ctx.oa,
          projectId: ctx.projectId,
          executionContractId: ctx.contract.executionContractId,
          forceLocalAuthority: true,
        });
        expect(confirmed.ok).toBe(true);
      }
      const authorized = await authorizeGenericPreparedContract({
        oa: ctx.oa,
        projectId: ctx.projectId,
        executionContractId: ctx.contract.executionContractId,
        expectedVersion: ctx.inspected.contractVersion,
        expectedSemanticFingerprint: ctx.inspected.semanticFingerprint,
        forceLocalAuthority: true,
      });
      expect(authorized.ok).toBe(true);
    });

    it("CM02 — materiality continuity refuses structural degradation", () => {
      const ok = materialityContinuityCompatible({
        sealed: "structural",
        current: "non_structural",
      });
      expect(ok.ok).toBe(false);
      if (ok.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
      expect(ok.reason).toBe("structural_materiality_degraded");
    });

    it("CM03 — historical signals absent + Product insufficient → REFUS path", async () => {
      const ctx = await prepareStructuralWithHd("cm03");
      const mutated = cloneMutated(ctx.contract, (c) => {
        const facts = {
          ...((c.inputs?.materialityFacts as object) ?? {}),
        } as Record<string, unknown>;
        delete facts.modelSignals;
        c.inputs = {
          ...c.inputs,
          impactSignals: null,
          materialityFacts: facts,
        };
      });
      // Clear sealed fallback by also using a contract whose verify still has HD
      // impactSignals from decision — to force unknown we need signals null AND
      // productContextInsufficient. Mutating scopeIn to empty breaks concrete paths.
      mutated.inputs = {
        ...mutated.inputs,
        scopeIn: [],
        missionScopeNarrative: "narrative without path",
      };
      const v = await verifyGenericMissionHumanDecisionForContract({
        oa: ctx.oa,
        projectId: ctx.projectId,
        decisionId: ctx.decision.decisionId,
        contract: mutated,
      });
      // Either scope mismatch vs HD or materiality unknown — both fail-closed.
      expect(v.ok).toBe(false);
    });

    it("CM05 — pathRoot Product changé → Confirm REFUS", async () => {
      const ctx = await prepareStructuralWithHd("cm05");
      await ctx.oa.projectServices.setProjectRepositoryBinding!.execute({
        projectId: ctx.projectId,
        actor: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
        binding: {
          provider: "github",
          identity: ctx.identity,
          remoteUrl: `https://github.com/${ctx.identity}.git`,
          defaultBranch: "main",
          pathRoot: "projects/other-root",
        },
      });
      const confirmed = await confirmExecutionContractForAuthorization({
        oa: ctx.oa,
        projectId: ctx.projectId,
        executionContractId: ctx.contract.executionContractId,
        forceLocalAuthority: true,
      });
      expect(confirmed.ok).toBe(false);
    });

    it("CM06 — NON_STRUCTURAL sans HD → non-régression", async () => {
      const { projectId, oa } = await seedProject("cm06");
      const analysis = buildIntentAnalysisFromSolicitedMission({
        objective: "Lecture bornée",
        expectedOutcome: "note",
        scope: "projects/demo/README.md",
        impactSignals: {
          structuralChange: false,
          architectureImpact: false,
          securityImpact: false,
          dataImpact: false,
          irreversible: false,
        },
      });
      const prep = await prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft({
        oa,
        projectId,
        cycleInstanceId: null,
        analysis,
        forceLocalAuthority: true,
        pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
      });
      expect(prep.ok).toBe(true);
      if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(prep.message);
      expect(prep.contract.decisionRefs ?? []).toEqual([]);
    });

    it("CM07 — unknown seal + structural current is compatible (documented)", () => {
      const ok = materialityContinuityCompatible({
        sealed: "unknown",
        current: "structural",
      });
      expect(ok.ok).toBe(true);
    });

    it("CM08 — cycle absent → parcours possible", async () => {
      const ctx = await prepareStructuralWithHd("cm08");
      expect(
        ctx.contract.cycleInstanceId == null ||
          ctx.contract.cycleInstanceId === "",
      ).toBe(true);
      const v = await verifyGenericMissionHumanDecisionForContract({
        oa: ctx.oa,
        projectId: ctx.projectId,
        decisionId: ctx.decision.decisionId,
        contract: ctx.contract,
      });
      expect(v.ok).toBe(true);
    });

    it("CM10 — HD superseded → Authorize REFUS", async () => {
      const ctx = await prepareStructuralWithHd("cm10");
      const resealed = await sealGenericMissionForPresentation({
        oa: ctx.oa,
        projectId: ctx.projectId,
        cycleInstanceId: null,
        analysis: ctx.analysis,
        missionSubjectId: ctx.decision.subject,
      });
      if (!resealed.ok) throw new Error(resealed.message);
      const second = await recordGenericMissionHumanDecision({
        oa: ctx.oa,
        subject: resealed.subject,
        selectedOptionId: GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_ACCEPT_PREPARE,
        analysis: ctx.analysis,
        forceLocalAuthority: true,
        supersedeExistingAccepted: true,
      });
      if (!second.ok) throw new Error(second.message);
      const authorized = await authorizeGenericPreparedContract({
        oa: ctx.oa,
        projectId: ctx.projectId,
        executionContractId: ctx.contract.executionContractId,
        expectedVersion: ctx.inspected.contractVersion,
        expectedSemanticFingerprint: ctx.inspected.semanticFingerprint,
        forceLocalAuthority: true,
      });
      expect(authorized.ok).toBe(false);
      if (authorized.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
      expect(authorized.code).toBe("HD_SUPERSEDED");
    });
  });
});
```

```
/**

import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

  GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REQUEST_AMEND,
} from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/genericMissionSubject";
import { mapGithubIdentityToPiloteActor } from "@/lib/auth/actorMapping";
import { LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR } from "@/lib/oa/decision";
import { ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton } from "@/lib/oa/project/infrastructure/managedRepoPathFacts";
import { SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime/managedRepoRootBaseConfig";
import {
  bootW2Runtime,
  cleanupW2TempDirs,
  tempProductDbPath,
  W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
} from "./w2Harness";
import type { RuntimeApplicationService } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime";
import type { IntentAnalysisDto } from "@/features/project-assistant/f2/types";

const APP = path.resolve(__dirname, "../..");

function structuralAnalysis(overrides?: {
  risks?: string[];
  stopConditions?: string[];
  reservations?: string[];
  structuralChange?: boolean;
}): IntentAnalysisDto {
  const base = buildIntentAnalysisFromSolicitedMission({
    objective: "Mission structurante correction M-HD-02",
    expectedOutcome: "Identité et continuité prouvées",
    scope: "projects/demo/src/mhd02-corr",
    impactSignals: {
      structuralChange: overrides?.structuralChange !== false,
      architectureImpact: true,
    },
  });
  return {
    ...base,
    risks: overrides?.risks ?? ["Risque A"],
    reservations: overrides?.reservations ?? ["Réserve A"],
    stopConditions: overrides?.stopConditions ?? ["STOP_A"],
    executionIntent: {
      intentKind: "code_change",
      scopeIn: ["projects/demo/src/mhd02-corr"],
      requiredCapabilities: [],
      requestedOperation: "refactor-corr",
      reversibilityExpectation: "reversible",
    },
  };
}

const SESSION_PILOTE = {
  actor: mapGithubIdentityToPiloteActor({
    githubUserId: "11111111",
    displayName: "Pilote QA",
  }),
  githubUserId: "11111111",
};

describe("P6 M-HD-02 mission identity / continuity / session / replay", () => {
  let runtime: RuntimeApplicationService;
  let previousMorris: string | undefined;
  let previousPilot: string | undefined;
  let previousDetPin: string | undefined;
  let previousSessionDb: string | undefined;

  beforeEach(() => {
    previousMorris = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY;
    previousPilot = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_LOCAL_PILOT_AUTHORITY;
    previousDetPin = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    previousSessionDb = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_NORA_SESSION_DB_PATH;
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY = "1";
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_LOCAL_PILOT_AUTHORITY = "1";
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA = W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    runtime = bootW2Runtime({
      productDbPath: tempProductDbPath("p6-mhd02-corr.sqlite"),
      idPrefix: "mhd02c",
    });
    resetPendingGenericPrepareOfferStoreForTests();
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    resetPendingGenericPrepareOfferStoreForTests();
    cleanupW2TempDirs();
    if (previousMorris === undefined) {
      delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY;
    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY = previousMorris;
    }
    if (previousPilot === undefined) {
      delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_LOCAL_PILOT_AUTHORITY;
    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_LOCAL_PILOT_AUTHORITY = previousPilot;
    }
    if (previousDetPin === undefined) {

    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA = previousDetPin;
    }
    if (previousSessionDb === undefined) {
      delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_NORA_SESSION_DB_PATH;
    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_NORA_SESSION_DB_PATH = previousSessionDb;
    }
  });

  async function seedProject(suffix: string) {
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const created = await runtime.createProject({
      name: `MHD02C ${suffix}`,
      objective: "M-HD-02 correction",
      context: "det mhd02c",
      criticality: "STANDARD",
      constraints: ["ZERO REAL"],
      shortReference: `MC${suffix.toUpperCase().slice(0, 6)}`,
      idempotencyKey: `idem:mhd02c-${suffix}`,
    });

    const projectId = created.project.projectId;

    await oa.projectServices.setProjectRepositoryBinding!.execute({
      projectId,
      actor: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,

      },
    });
    ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton({

    return { projectId, oa };
```

```
    scope: "projects/demo/src/mhd02",

  });
});
```

```
/**

 * Option 1 — W2 continuity admits G-D4 NON_STRUCTURAL empty decisionRefs.
```
