```
 * @vitest-environment node
 */
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { resetF2ProposalStoreForTests } from "@/features/project-assistant/f2/proposalStore";
import type { IntentAnalysisDto } from "@/features/project-assistant/f2/types";
import { prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft";
import { readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity";
import { inspectExecutionContract } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/inspectExecutionContract";
import { resetPendingGenericPrepareOfferStoreForTests } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/pendingGenericPrepareOfferStore";
import {
  LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
  registerLocalPiloteAuthority,
} from "@/lib/oa/decision";
import { setConversationProviderForTests } from "@/lib/platform/ai";
import { ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton } from "@/lib/oa/project/infrastructure/managedRepoPathFacts";
import { SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime/managedRepoRootBaseConfig";
import {
  bootW2Runtime,
  cleanupW2TempDirs,
  tempProductDbPath,
  W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
} from "./w2Harness";
import type { RuntimeApplicationService } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime";

function restoreEnvVar(name: string, previous: string | undefined): void {
  if (previous === undefined) delete process.env[name];
  else process.env[name] = previous;
}

function baseAnalysis(
  overrides: Partial<IntentAnalysisDto> = {},
): IntentAnalysisDto {
  return {
    parseOk: true,
    intentClass: "actionable",
    candidateCycleTypeId: "cyc:delivery",
    signals: {
      structuralChange: false,
      securityImpact: false,
      architectureImpact: false,
      dataImpact: false,
      irreversible: false,
      lowRiskBounded: true,
    },
    cognitiveWorkload: null,
    contradictionCandidate: null,
    challengeResponseAssessment: "sufficient",
    objective: "Corriger la fonctionnalité de filtrage",
    scope: "projects/demo/app/filter.ts",
    rephrasedRequest: "Corriger le bug de filtrage signalé",
    outOfScope: ["refonte UI", "migration DB"],
    risks: [],
    reservations: [],
    stopConditions: ["AUCUNE EXÉCUTION SANS AUTORISATION"],
    activatedBlocks: [],
    expectedOutcome: "Filtrage corrigé avec tests verts",
    criticalJustification: null,
    requestedOperation: null,
    executionIntent: {
      intentKind: "other",
      scopeIn: ["projects/demo/app/filter.ts"],
      scopeOut: ["refonte UI"],
    } as IntentAnalysisDto["executionIntent"],
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe("P6 O1 — continuity G-D4 empty decisionRefs", () => {
  let runtime: RuntimeApplicationService;
  let dbPath: string;
  let previousProvider: string | undefined;
  let previousMorrisAuthority: string | undefined;

  beforeEach(() => {
    previousProvider = process.env.OPS1_CONVERSATION_PROVIDER;
    previousMorrisAuthority = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY;
    process.env.OPS1_CONVERSATION_PROVIDER = "fake";
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY = "1";
    setConversationProviderForTests(null);
    resetF2ProposalStoreForTests();
    resetPendingGenericPrepareOfferStoreForTests();
    dbPath = tempProductDbPath("p6-o1-cont.sqlite");
    runtime = bootW2Runtime({ productDbPath: dbPath, idPrefix: "o1c" });
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    resetF2ProposalStoreForTests();
    resetPendingGenericPrepareOfferStoreForTests();
    setConversationProviderForTests(null);
    cleanupW2TempDirs();
    restoreEnvVar("OPS1_CONVERSATION_PROVIDER", previousProvider);
    restoreEnvVar(
      "SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY",
      previousMorrisAuthority,
    );
  });

  async function seedActiveCycle(suffix: string): Promise<{
    projectId: string;
    cycleInstanceId: string;
  }> {
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const created = await runtime.createProject({
      name: `O1 Cont ${suffix}`,
      objective: "Continuité G-D4 empty refs",
      context: "active cycle + generic prepare",
      criticality: "STANDARD",
      constraints: ["ZERO REAL"],
      shortReference: `O1C${suffix.toUpperCase().slice(0, 6)}`,
      idempotencyKey: `idem:o1c-${suffix}`,
    });
    expect(created.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!created.ok) throw new Error("createProject");
    const projectId = created.project.projectId;

    const lps0 = await oa.projectServices.getCurrentLivingProjectState.execute({
      projectId,
    });
    if (!lps0.ok) throw new Error("lps0");

    const traj = await oa.cycleServices.createInitialTrajectory.execute({
      trajectoryId: `trj:${projectId}`,
      projectId,
      steps: [
        { stepId: "stp:a", order: 1, label: "Clarify", state: "done" },
        { stepId: "stp:b", order: 2, label: "Deliver", state: "done" },
      ],
      status: "active",
      expectedLpsVersion: lps0.livingProjectState.version,
      createdBy: {
        actorId: "actor:morris",
        role: "project_owner",
        displayName: "Morris",
        authorityLevel: "N3",
      },
    });
    expect(traj.ok).toBe(true);

    const cycleInstanceId = `cyc:o1c-${suffix}`;
    const candidate = await oa.cycleServices.createCycle.execute({
      cycleInstanceId,
      cycleTypeId: "cyc:delivery",
      projectId,
      signals: { lowRiskBounded: true },
      createdBy: {
        actorId: "actor:nora-f2",
        role: "agent",
        displayName: "Nora F2",
        authorityLevel: "N1",
      },
      linkAsActiveCycle: false,
    });
    expect(candidate.ok).toBe(true);

    const auth = registerLocalPiloteAuthority({
      authorityResolver: oa.authorityResolver,
      scope: `pilot-lifecycle:${cycleInstanceId}`,
      issuedAt: "2026-10-15T08:00:00.000Z",
      forceEnable: true,
    });
    expect(auth.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!auth.ok) throw new Error("auth");

    const lps1 = await oa.projectServices.getCurrentLivingProjectState.execute({
      projectId,
    });
    if (!lps1.ok) throw new Error("lps1");

    const started = await oa.cycleServices.pilotLifecycle.start({
      cycleInstanceId,
      projectId,
      createdBy: {
        actorId: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.actorId,
        role: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.role,
        displayName: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.displayName,
        authorityLevel: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.authorityLevel,
      },
      authorityEvidenceId: auth.evidenceId,
      expectedLpsVersion: lps1.livingProjectState.version,
    });
    expect(started.ok).toBe(true);

    if (oa.projectServices.setProjectRepositoryBinding) {
      const identity = `acme/o1c-${suffix}`;
      const bound = await oa.projectServices.setProjectRepositoryBinding.execute({
        projectId,
        actor: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
        binding: {
          provider: "github",
          identity,
          remoteUrl: `https://github.com/${identity}.git`,
          defaultBranch: "main",
          pathRoot: "projects/demo",
        },
      });
      expect(bound.ok).toBe(true);
      ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton({
        managedRepoRootBase: process.env[SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV]!,
        identity,
      });
    }

    return { projectId, cycleInstanceId };
  }

  async function prepareOk(
    projectId: string,
    cycleInstanceId: string,
    analysis: IntentAnalysisDto = baseAnalysis(),
  ) {
    const prep = await prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft({
      oa: runtime.oa!,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId,
      analysis,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
    });
    expect(prep.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(prep.message);
    return prep;
  }

  it("T1/T14/T15 — G-D4 NON_STRUCTURAL empty refs → continuity active decisionRef=null", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId } = await seedActiveCycle("t1");
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const beforeHd = await oa.decisionServices.decisions.listByProject(projectId);

    const prep = await prepareOk(projectId, cycleInstanceId);
    expect(prep.humanDecisionCreated).toBe(false);
    expect(prep.executionAttemptCreated).toBe(false);
    expect(prep.contract.decisionRefs ?? []).toEqual([]);
    expect(prep.contract.constraints).toContain("MATERIALITY:NON_STRUCTURAL");

    const continuity = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(continuity.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!continuity.ok) throw new Error(continuity.message);
    expect(continuity.kind).toBe("active");
    if (continuity.kind !== "active") throw new Error("expected active");
    expect(continuity.decisionRef).toBeNull();
    expect(continuity.contract.executionContractId).toBe(
      prep.contract.executionContractId,
    );
    expect(continuity.contract.version).toBe(prep.contract.version);
    expect(continuity.contract.semanticFingerprint).toBe(
      prep.contract.semanticFingerprint ?? "",
    );

    const afterHd = await oa.decisionServices.decisions.listByProject(projectId);
    expect(afterHd.length).toBe(beforeHd.length);

    const attempts = await oa.executionAttemptServices!.listExecutionAttempts.execute(
      { executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId },
    );
    expect(attempts.ok).toBe(true);
    if (attempts.ok) expect(attempts.attempts).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("T4 — empty refs without MATERIALITY stamp → INTEGRITY refus (G5 preserved)", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId } = await seedActiveCycle("t4");
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const prep = await prepareOk(projectId, cycleInstanceId);

    const loaded = await oa.executionContractServices.getExecutionContract.execute({
      executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
    });
    expect(loaded.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!loaded.ok) throw new Error("load");
    const stripped = {
      ...loaded.contract,
      decisionRefs: [] as string[],
      constraints: loaded.contract.constraints.filter(
        (c) => !c.startsWith("MATERIALITY:"),
      ),
    };
    await oa.executionContractServices.contracts.save(stripped);

    const continuity = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(continuity.ok).toBe(false);
    if (continuity.ok) throw new Error("expected fail");
    expect(continuity.code).toBe("EXECUTION_CONTINUITY_INTEGRITY_FAILED");
  });

  it("T2 — STRUCTURAL stamp + empty refs → refus", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId } = await seedActiveCycle("t2");
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const prep = await prepareOk(projectId, cycleInstanceId);
    const loaded = await oa.executionContractServices.getExecutionContract.execute({
      executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
    });
    if (!loaded.ok) throw new Error("load");
    const structural = {
      ...loaded.contract,
      decisionRefs: [] as string[],
      constraints: [
        ...loaded.contract.constraints.filter((c) => !c.startsWith("MATERIALITY:")),
        "MATERIALITY:STRUCTURAL",
      ],
    };
    await oa.executionContractServices.contracts.save(structural);

    const continuity = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(continuity.ok).toBe(false);
    if (continuity.ok) throw new Error("expected fail");
    expect(
      continuity.code === "MATERIALITY_STRUCTURAL" ||
        continuity.code === "MATERIALITY_CURRENT_MISMATCH" ||
        continuity.code === "EXECUTION_CONTINUITY_INTEGRITY_FAILED",
    ).toBe(true);
  });

  it("T5a — NON_STRUCTURAL stamp but missing productCurrentness facts → refus", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId } = await seedActiveCycle("t5a");
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const prep = await prepareOk(projectId, cycleInstanceId);
    const loaded = await oa.executionContractServices.getExecutionContract.execute({
      executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
    });
    if (!loaded.ok) throw new Error("load");
    const inputs = { ...(loaded.contract.inputs ?? {}) } as Record<string, unknown>;
    const facts = {
      ...((inputs.materialityFacts as Record<string, unknown>) ?? {}),
    };
    delete facts.productCurrentness;
    inputs.materialityFacts = facts;
    await oa.executionContractServices.contracts.save({
      ...loaded.contract,
      inputs,
    });

    const continuity = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(continuity.ok).toBe(false);
    if (continuity.ok) throw new Error("expected fail");
    expect(continuity.code).toBe("EXECUTION_CONTINUITY_INTEGRITY_FAILED");
  });

  it("T5 — true Product obsolescence (pathRoot change) → CURRENT/STALE refus", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId } = await seedActiveCycle("t5");
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const prep = await prepareOk(projectId, cycleInstanceId);

    const before = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(before.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!before.ok || before.kind !== "active") throw new Error("expected active");

    const identity = `acme/o1c-t5`;
    const rebound = await oa.projectServices.setProjectRepositoryBinding!.execute({
      projectId,
      actor: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
      binding: {
        provider: "github",
        identity,
        remoteUrl: `https://github.com/${identity}.git`,
        defaultBranch: "main",
        pathRoot: "projects/demo-alt",
      },
    });
    expect(rebound.ok).toBe(true);

    const continuity = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(continuity.ok).toBe(false);
    if (continuity.ok) throw new Error("expected stale refus");
    expect(
      continuity.code === "REPOSITORY_BINDING_STALE" ||
        continuity.code === "MATERIALITY_CURRENTNESS_STALE" ||
        continuity.code === "MATERIALITY_CURRENT_MISMATCH" ||
        continuity.code === "EXECUTION_CONTINUITY_INTEGRITY_FAILED",
    ).toBe(true);
  });

  it("T3 — CURRENT materiality UNKNOWN + empty refs → continuity refus", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId } = await seedActiveCycle("t3");
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const prep = await prepareOk(projectId, cycleInstanceId);
    const loaded = await oa.executionContractServices.getExecutionContract.execute({
      executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
    });
    if (!loaded.ok) throw new Error("load");
    // Force stamp UNKNOWN while keeping empty refs — never admit without HD.
    await oa.executionContractServices.contracts.save({
      ...loaded.contract,
      decisionRefs: [],
      constraints: [
        ...loaded.contract.constraints.filter((c) => !c.startsWith("MATERIALITY:")),
        "MATERIALITY:UNKNOWN",
      ],
    });

    const continuity = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(continuity.ok).toBe(false);
    if (continuity.ok) throw new Error("expected UNKNOWN refus");
    expect(
      continuity.code === "MATERIALITY_UNKNOWN" ||
        continuity.code === "MATERIALITY_CURRENT_MISMATCH" ||
        continuity.code === "EXECUTION_CONTINUITY_INTEGRITY_FAILED",
    ).toBe(true);
  });

  it("T11/T12/T13 — inspection + amend fingerprint requires reinspect; confirm refused", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId } = await seedActiveCycle("t11");
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const prep = await prepareOk(projectId, cycleInstanceId);

    const beforeInspect = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(beforeInspect.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!beforeInspect.ok || beforeInspect.kind !== "active") {
      throw new Error("expected active");
    }
    expect(beforeInspect.inspection.inspectionSufficient).toBe(false);

    const inspected = await inspectExecutionContract({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
      expectedVersion: prep.contract.version,
    });
    expect(inspected.ok).toBe(true);

    const afterInspect = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(afterInspect.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!afterInspect.ok || afterInspect.kind !== "active") {
      throw new Error("expected active");
    }
    expect(afterInspect.inspection.inspectionSufficient).toBe(true);
    expect(afterInspect.decisionRef).toBeNull();
    expect(afterInspect.contract.status).toBe(prep.contract.status);

    const { amendExecutionContractWithConstraint } = await import(
      "@/features/project-assistant/w2/amendExecutionContract"
    );
    const amended = await amendExecutionContractWithConstraint({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
      additionalConstraint: "PASS02:T13-FP-BUMP",
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(amended.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!amended.ok) throw new Error(amended.message);

    const afterAmend = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(afterAmend.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!afterAmend.ok || afterAmend.kind !== "active") {
      throw new Error("expected active after amend");
    }
    // Successor (or bumped) contract must not reuse prior inspection silently.
    expect(afterAmend.inspection.inspectionSufficient).toBe(false);
    expect(
      afterAmend.inspection.reinspectionRequired === true ||
        afterAmend.contract.executionContractId !==
          prep.contract.executionContractId ||
        afterAmend.contract.version !== prep.contract.version ||
        afterAmend.contract.semanticFingerprint !==
          (prep.contract.semanticFingerprint ?? ""),
    ).toBe(true);

    const { confirmExecutionContractForAuthorization } = await import(
      "@/features/project-assistant/w2/confirmForAuthorization"
    );
    const confirmed = await confirmExecutionContractForAuthorization({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: afterAmend.contract.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(confirmed.ok).toBe(false);
    if (confirmed.ok) throw new Error("expected confirm refus before reinspect");
  });

  it("T16 — continuity read performs no EC / HD / Inspection / Confirm / Attempt writes", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId } = await seedActiveCycle("t16");
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const prep = await prepareOk(projectId, cycleInstanceId);

    const saveSpy = vi.spyOn(oa.executionContractServices.contracts, "save");
    const hdBefore = await oa.decisionServices.decisions.listByProject(projectId);
    const inspBefore =
      await oa.executionContractServices.inspectionAttestations.listByContract(
        prep.contract.executionContractId,
      );
    const attemptsBefore =
      await oa.executionAttemptServices!.listExecutionAttempts.execute({
        executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
      });

    const continuity = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(continuity.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!continuity.ok || continuity.kind !== "active") {
      throw new Error("expected active");
    }
    expect(continuity.contract.executionContractId).toBe(
      prep.contract.executionContractId,
    );
    expect(saveSpy).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    const hdAfter = await oa.decisionServices.decisions.listByProject(projectId);
    expect(hdAfter.length).toBe(hdBefore.length);
    const inspAfter =
      await oa.executionContractServices.inspectionAttestations.listByContract(
        prep.contract.executionContractId,
      );
    expect(inspAfter.length).toBe(inspBefore.length);
    const attemptsAfter =
      await oa.executionAttemptServices!.listExecutionAttempts.execute({
        executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
      });
    expect(attemptsAfter.ok).toBe(true);
    if (attemptsBefore.ok && attemptsAfter.ok) {
      expect(attemptsAfter.attempts.length).toBe(attemptsBefore.attempts.length);
    }
    saveSpy.mockRestore();
  });
});
```

```
/**
 * P6 — Project-scoped generic Cursor selection / execution Delivery.
 *
 * Proves (deterministic Fake Cursor boundary — ZERO Cursor REAL / OpenAI LIVE):
 *  - CycleInstance optional on AUTHORIZED PRODUCT_GOVERNED EC → Select/Start/Result
 *  - Methodological profile is NOT an access gate to Cursor Generalist
 *  - Project positive binding (missing / archived → fail-closed)
 *  - Cycle-bound EC keeps lifecycle gates (closed cycle → refuse)
 *  - No invented CycleInstance / cycleTypeId
 *  - Structural HD still fail-closed at PREPARE; HD–mission resume remains OPEN debt
 *
 * @vitest-environment node
 */
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import {
  governedExecuteSelectAgent,
  governedExecuteStart,
} from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/governedExecuteAuthorizedContract";
import { solicitGenericExecutionPrepare } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft";
import { inspectExecutionContract } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/inspectExecutionContract";
import { authorizeGenericPreparedContract } from "@/features/project-assistant/w2/authorizeGenericPreparedContract";
import {
  LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
  registerLocalPiloteAuthority,
} from "@/lib/oa/decision";
import { STUDIO_CURSOR_GENERALIST_AGENT_ID } from "@/lib/oa/execution-attempt";
import { ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton } from "@/lib/oa/project/infrastructure/managedRepoPathFacts";
import { SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime/managedRepoRootBaseConfig";
import {
  bootW2Runtime,
  cleanupW2TempDirs,
  settleDeterministicProductCursorSuccess,
  tempProductDbPath,
  W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
} from "./w2Harness";
import type { RuntimeApplicationService } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime";

describe("P6 project-scoped Cursor selection Delivery", () => {
  let runtime: RuntimeApplicationService;
  let sessionDbPath: string;
  let previousMorris: string | undefined;
  let previousDetPin: string | undefined;
  let previousCursorReal: string | undefined;

  beforeEach(() => {
    previousMorris = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY;
    previousDetPin = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    previousCursorReal = process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_CURSOR_REAL;
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY = "1";
    process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA = W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    // Fake Select→Start path: REAL readiness must stay unset (ZERO Cursor REAL).
    // Host shells with SFIA_STUDIO_CURSOR_REAL=1 otherwise hit PRODUCT_DB_PROJECT_NOT_FOUND.
    delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_CURSOR_REAL;
    sessionDbPath = tempProductDbPath("p6-pscs.sqlite");
    runtime = bootW2Runtime({
      productDbPath: sessionDbPath,
      idPrefix: "pscs",
    });
  });

  afterEach(() => {
    cleanupW2TempDirs();
    if (previousMorris === undefined) {
      delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY;
    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_M3_LOCAL_MORRIS_AUTHORITY = previousMorris;
    }
    if (previousDetPin === undefined) {
      delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA;
    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_DET_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA = previousDetPin;
    }
    if (previousCursorReal === undefined) {
      delete process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_CURSOR_REAL;
    } else {
      process.env.SFIA_STUDIO_CURSOR_REAL = previousCursorReal;
    }
  });

  async function seedProject(input: {
    suffix: string;
    withCycle: boolean;
    cycleProfile?: "Standard" | "Critical";
  }) {
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const created = await runtime.createProject({
      name: `PSCS ${input.suffix}`,
      objective: "Project-scoped Cursor selection",
      context: "det pscs",
      criticality: "STANDARD",
      constraints: ["ZERO REAL"],
      shortReference: `PS${input.suffix.toUpperCase().slice(0, 6)}`,
      idempotencyKey: `idem:pscs-${input.suffix}`,
    });
    if (!created.ok) throw new Error("createProject");
    const projectId = created.project.projectId;
    const lps0 = await oa.projectServices.getCurrentLivingProjectState.execute({
      projectId,
    });
    if (!lps0.ok) throw new Error("lps0");
    await oa.cycleServices.createInitialTrajectory.execute({
      trajectoryId: `trj:${projectId}`,
      projectId,
      steps: [{ stepId: "stp:a", order: 1, label: "A", state: "done" }],
      status: "active",
      expectedLpsVersion: lps0.livingProjectState.version,
      createdBy: {
        actorId: "actor:morris",
        role: "project_owner",
        displayName: "Morris",
        authorityLevel: "N3",
      },
    });

    let cycleInstanceId: string | null = null;
    if (input.withCycle) {
      cycleInstanceId = `cyc:pscs-${input.suffix}`;
      await oa.cycleServices.createCycle.execute({
        cycleInstanceId,
        cycleTypeId: "cyc:delivery",
        projectId,
        signals:
          input.cycleProfile === "Critical"
            ? { irreversible: true }
            : { lowRiskBounded: true },
        justification:
          input.cycleProfile === "Critical"
            ? "Signal irréversible pour preuve Critical-cycle"
            : undefined,
        createdBy: {
          actorId: "actor:nora-f2",
          role: "agent",
          displayName: "Nora F2",
          authorityLevel: "N1",
        },
        linkAsActiveCycle: false,
      });
      const auth = registerLocalPiloteAuthority({
        authorityResolver: oa.authorityResolver,
        scope: `pilot-lifecycle:${cycleInstanceId}`,
        issuedAt: "2026-10-16T10:00:00.000Z",
        forceEnable: true,
      });
      if (!auth.ok) throw new Error("auth");
      const lps1 = await oa.projectServices.getCurrentLivingProjectState.execute({
        projectId,
      });
      if (!lps1.ok) throw new Error("lps1");
      const started = await oa.cycleServices.pilotLifecycle.start({
        cycleInstanceId,
        projectId,
        createdBy: {
          actorId: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.actorId,
          role: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.role,
          displayName: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.displayName,
          authorityLevel: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.authorityLevel,
        },
        authorityEvidenceId: auth.evidenceId,
        expectedLpsVersion: lps1.livingProjectState.version,
      });
      if (!started.ok) throw new Error("start");
    }

    const identity = `acme/pscs-${input.suffix}`;
    await oa.projectServices.setProjectRepositoryBinding!.execute({
      projectId,
      actor: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
      binding: {
        provider: "github",
        identity,
        remoteUrl: `https://github.com/${identity}.git`,
        defaultBranch: "main",
        pathRoot: "projects/demo",
      },
    });
    ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton({
      managedRepoRootBase: process.env[SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV]!,
      identity,
    });
    return { projectId, cycleInstanceId, oa };
  }

  async function prepareAuthorize(input: {
    projectId: string;
    oa: NonNullable<RuntimeApplicationService["oa"]>;
    cycleInstanceId: string | null;
    objective?: string;
    scope?: string;
  }) {
    const prep = await solicitGenericExecutionPrepare({
      oa: input.oa,
      projectId: input.projectId,
      cycleInstanceId: input.cycleInstanceId,
      mission: {
        objective:
          input.objective ?? "Refactorer le module d'export du projet demo",
        expectedOutcome: "Module d'export simplifié",
        scope: input.scope ?? "projects/demo/src",
      },
      sessionDbPath,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
    });
    if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(`prep ${prep.code}: ${prep.message}`);
    const inspected = await inspectExecutionContract({
      oa: input.oa,
      projectId: input.projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
    });
    if (!inspected.ok) throw new Error(`inspect ${inspected.message}`);
    const authorized = await authorizeGenericPreparedContract({
      oa: input.oa,
      projectId: input.projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      expectedVersion: inspected.contractVersion,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: inspected.semanticFingerprint,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    if (!authorized.ok) {
      throw new Error(`authorize ${authorized.code}: ${authorized.message}`);
    }
    return { prep, inspected, authorized };
  }

  async function countCycles(
    oa: NonNullable<RuntimeApplicationService["oa"]>,
    projectId: string,
  ): Promise<number> {
    return (await oa.cycleServices.cycles.listByProject(projectId)).length;
  }

  async function countAttempts(
    oa: NonNullable<RuntimeApplicationService["oa"]>,
    projectId: string,
  ): Promise<number> {
    const contracts = await oa.executionContractServices.contracts.listByProject(
      projectId,
    );
    let total = 0;
    for (const c of contracts) {
      total += (
        await oa.executionAttemptServices!.attempts.listByContract(
          c.executionContractId,
        )
      ).length;
    }
    return total;
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // S1 / S8 / S19 — Project, no cycle, no methodological profile gate
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  it("S1/S8/S19 — Project actif sans cycle: PREPARE + Select/Start/Result Cursor Generalist (no profile gate)", async () => {
    const { projectId, oa } = await seedProject({
      suffix: "s1",
      withCycle: false,
    });
    const cyclesBefore = await countCycles(oa, projectId);
    expect(cyclesBefore).toBe(0);

    const { prep } = await prepareAuthorize({
      projectId,
      oa,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
    });
    expect(prep.cycleInstanceId ?? null).toBeNull();

    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    if (!selected.ok) {
      throw new Error(`S1 select failed: ${selected.code} — ${selected.message}`);
    }
    expect(selected.phase).toBe("accepted");
    expect(selected.selectedAgentRef).toBe(STUDIO_CURSOR_GENERALIST_AGENT_ID);
    // Technical OA carrier for capabilities_deterministic — not methodological Standard claim.
    expect(selected.selectionProfile).toBe("standard");

    const started = await governedExecuteStart({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      attemptId: selected.attemptId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(started.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!started.ok) throw new Error(started.message);
    expect(started.phase).toBe("running");

    const settled = await settleDeterministicProductCursorSuccess({
      oa,
      attemptId: started.attemptId,
    });
    expect(settled.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!settled.ok) throw new Error(settled.message);
    // OA Attempt succeeded = Select → Start → Result continuity (Fake boundary).
    expect(settled.attempt.status).toBe("succeeded");

    const after = await oa.executionContractServices.getExecutionContract.execute({
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
    });
    expect(after.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!after.ok) throw new Error("get");
    expect(after.contract.cycleInstanceId ?? null).toBeNull();
    expect(await countCycles(oa, projectId)).toBe(0);
    expect(await countAttempts(oa, projectId)).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // S2 / S3 / S9 — with cycle non-regression
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  it("S2/S9 — Project + cycle Standard: Select Cursor Generalist (non-regression)", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId, oa } = await seedProject({
      suffix: "s2",
      withCycle: true,
      cycleProfile: "Standard",
    });
    expect(cycleInstanceId).toBeTruthy();
    const { prep } = await prepareAuthorize({
      projectId,
      oa,
      cycleInstanceId,
    });
    expect(prep.cycleInstanceId).toBe(cycleInstanceId);

    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!selected.ok) throw new Error(selected.message);
    expect(selected.selectedAgentRef).toBe(STUDIO_CURSOR_GENERALIST_AGENT_ID);
    // Cursor REAL Product path: capability selection — not Critical/Standard access gate.
    expect(selected.selectionProfile).toBe("standard");
  });

  it("S3 — Project + cycle Critical: Cursor access still Select (profile ≠ tool access gate)", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId, oa } = await seedProject({
      suffix: "s3",
      withCycle: true,
      cycleProfile: "Critical",
    });
    const { prep } = await prepareAuthorize({
      projectId,
      oa,
      cycleInstanceId,
      objective: "Mission Critical-cycle Project-scoped Cursor (effects-governed)",
    });

    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!selected.ok) throw new Error(selected.message);
    expect(selected.selectedAgentRef).toBe(STUDIO_CURSOR_GENERALIST_AGENT_ID);
    expect(selected.selectionProfile).toBe("standard");
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // S4 / S20 — closed cycle on Project; new independent mission without cycle
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  it("S4/S20 — dernier cycle clos: mission Project indépendante (null cycle) OK; cycle clos non muté", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId, oa } = await seedProject({
      suffix: "s4",
      withCycle: true,
    });
    expect(cycleInstanceId).toBeTruthy();
    const got = await oa.cycleServices.getCycle.execute({
      cycleInstanceId: cycleInstanceId!,
    });
    if (!got.ok) throw new Error("cycle");
    const closed = { ...got.cycle, status: "completed" as const };
    await oa.cycleServices.cycles.save(closed);

    const cyclesBefore = await countCycles(oa, projectId);
    const { prep } = await prepareAuthorize({
      projectId,
      oa,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
      objective: "Mission indépendante après cycle clos",
    });
    expect(prep.cycleInstanceId ?? null).toBeNull();

    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!selected.ok) throw new Error(selected.message);

    const afterCycle = await oa.cycleServices.getCycle.execute({
      cycleInstanceId: cycleInstanceId!,
    });
    expect(afterCycle.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!afterCycle.ok) throw new Error("cycle after");
    expect(afterCycle.cycle.status).toBe("completed");
    expect(await countCycles(oa, projectId)).toBe(cyclesBefore);
  });

  it("S20b — EC still bound to completed cycle → CYCLE_NOT_ACTIVE (no closed-cycle mutation)", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId, oa } = await seedProject({
      suffix: "s20b",
      withCycle: true,
    });
    const { prep } = await prepareAuthorize({
      projectId,
      oa,
      cycleInstanceId,
    });
    const got = await oa.cycleServices.getCycle.execute({
      cycleInstanceId: cycleInstanceId!,
    });
    if (!got.ok) throw new Error("cycle");
    await oa.cycleServices.cycles.save({
      ...got.cycle,
      status: "completed",
    });

    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("CYCLE_NOT_ACTIVE");
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // S5 / S6 / S7 — Project negative binding
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  it("S5 — Project introuvable → PROJECT_NOT_FOUND (fail-closed, no Attempt)", async () => {
    const { projectId, oa } = await seedProject({
      suffix: "s5",
      withCycle: false,
    });
    const { prep } = await prepareAuthorize({
      projectId,
      oa,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
    });
    const spy = vi
      .spyOn(oa.projectServices.getProject, "execute")
      .mockResolvedValue({
        ok: false,
        error: {
          code: "STATE_CONFLICT",
          detailCode: "PROJECT_NOT_FOUND",
          message: "missing",
          severity: "error",
          retryable: false,
          blocking: true,
          recoverable: false,
          domain: "C",
          timestamp: "2026-10-16T10:00:00.000Z",
          correlationId: "cor:test",
        },
      } as never);

    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    spy.mockRestore();
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("PROJECT_NOT_FOUND");
    expect(await countAttempts(oa, projectId)).toBe(0);
  });

  it("S6 — Project archivé → PROJECT_ARCHIVED", async () => {
    const { projectId, oa } = await seedProject({
      suffix: "s6",
      withCycle: false,
    });
    const { prep } = await prepareAuthorize({
      projectId,
      oa,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
    });
    const got = await oa.projectServices.getProject.execute({ projectId });
    if (!got.ok) throw new Error("get");
    await oa.projectServices.projects.save({
      ...got.project,
      status: "archived",
    });

    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("PROJECT_ARCHIVED");
  });

  it("S7 — Project mismatch → PROJECT_MISMATCH", async () => {
    const a = await seedProject({ suffix: "s7a", withCycle: false });
    const b = await seedProject({ suffix: "s7b", withCycle: false });
    const { prep } = await prepareAuthorize({
      projectId: a.projectId,
      oa: a.oa,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
    });
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa: a.oa,
      projectId: b.projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(false);
    if (selected.ok) throw new Error("expected refuse");
    expect(selected.code).toBe("PROJECT_MISMATCH");
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // S10 / S11 / S12 — effects / HD
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  it("S10 — mission faible effet: PREPARE + Select proportionnés (no cycle)", async () => {
    const { projectId, oa } = await seedProject({
      suffix: "s10",
      withCycle: false,
    });
    const { prep } = await prepareAuthorize({
      projectId,
      oa,
      cycleInstanceId: null,

      scope: "projects/demo/src/exports",
    });
    const selected = await governedExecuteSelectAgent({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(selected.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!selected.ok) throw new Error(selected.message);
    expect(selected.selectedAgentRef).toBe(STUDIO_CURSOR_GENERALIST_AGENT_ID);
  });

  it("S12 — mission structurante sans HD → fail-closed at PREPARE (no Select)", async () => {
    const { projectId, oa } = await seedProject({
      suffix: "s12",
      withCycle: false,
    });
    const prep = await solicitGenericExecutionPrepare({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
      mission: {
        objective: "Refactor structurel API publique",
        expectedOutcome: "API publique simplifiée",
        scope: "projects/demo/src",
        impactSignals: { structuralChange: true },
      },
      sessionDbPath,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
    });
    expect(prep.ok).toBe(false);
    if (prep.ok) throw new Error("expected structural refuse");
    expect(prep.code).toBe("STRUCTURAL_HUMAN_DECISION_REQUIRED");
    expect(await countAttempts(oa, projectId)).toBe(0);
  });

  it("S13 — OPEN debt: HD–mission structural resume NOT PROVEN (no silent unlock)", async () => {
    // Documented OPEN: Generic PREPARE has decisionRefs:[] — a Project HD does
    // not bind to a mission. Positive path remains NOT PROVEN (der16 R5).
    expect(true).toBe(true);
  });

  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
  // S14 / S15 / S16 / S17 / S18
  // -------------------------------------------------------------------------

  it("S15 — EC superseded/stale authorize fingerprint → refuse before Select", async () => {
    const { projectId, oa } = await seedProject({
      suffix: "s15",
      withCycle: false,
    });
    const prep = await solicitGenericExecutionPrepare({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
      mission: {
        objective: "Mission fingerprint stale",
        expectedOutcome: "n/a",
        scope: "projects/demo/src",
      },
      sessionDbPath,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
    });
    if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(prep.message);
    const inspected = await inspectExecutionContract({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
    });
    if (!inspected.ok) throw new Error(inspected.message);
    const authorized = await authorizeGenericPreparedContract({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
      expectedVersion: inspected.contractVersion,
      expectedSemanticFingerprint: "sha256:deadbeef".padEnd(71, "0"),
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(authorized.ok).toBe(false);
  });

  it("S17/S18 — Artifact demand / code mission: Generic PREPARE (no docs_write taxonomy gate)", async () => {
    const { projectId, oa } = await seedProject({
      suffix: "s17",
      withCycle: false,
    });
    const prep = await solicitGenericExecutionPrepare({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId: null,
      mission: {
        objective: "Écrire un test unitaire pour le module export",
        expectedOutcome: "Test unitaire ajouté",
        scope: "projects/demo/src",
      },
      sessionDbPath,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
    });
    expect(prep.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(prep.message);
    const loaded = await oa.executionContractServices.getExecutionContract.execute({
      executionContractId: prep.executionContractId,
    });
    expect(loaded.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!loaded.ok) throw new Error("get");
    expect(loaded.contract.constraints).toContain("PRODUCT_GOVERNED");
    expect(loaded.contract.constraints).toContain("GENERIC_MISSION_NO_TASK_CATALOG");
    expect(loaded.contract.cycleInstanceId ?? null).toBeNull();
  });

  it("static — loadContract no longer hard-gates on missing cycleInstanceId", async () => {
    const fs = await import("node:fs");
    const path = await import("node:path");
    const src = fs.readFileSync(
      path.resolve(
        __dirname,
        "../../features/project-assistant/w2/governedExecuteAuthorizedContract.ts",
      ),
      "utf8",
    );
    expect(src).not.toMatch(/CYCLE_BINDING_REQUIRED/);
    expect(src).toMatch(/PROJECT_NOT_FOUND/);
    expect(src).toMatch(/Project-scoped generic/);
  });
});
```

```
}

      projectId,
    });
    if (!lps1.ok) throw new Error("lps1");

    await oa.cycleServices.pilotLifecycle.start({
      cycleInstanceId,
      projectId,
      createdBy: {
        actorId: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.actorId,
        role: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.role,
        displayName: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.displayName,
        authorityLevel: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR.authorityLevel,
      },
      authorityEvidenceId: auth.evidenceId,
      expectedLpsVersion: lps1.livingProjectState.version,
    });

    if (oa.projectServices.setProjectRepositoryBinding) {
      const identity = `acme/r03i-${suffix}`;
      await oa.projectServices.setProjectRepositoryBinding.execute({
        projectId,
        actor: LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
        binding: {
          provider: "github",
          identity,
          remoteUrl: `https://github.com/${identity}.git`,
          defaultBranch: "main",
          pathRoot: "projects/demo",
        },
      });
      ensureManagedRepoCloneSkeleton({
        managedRepoRootBase: process.env[SFIA_STUDIO_MANAGED_REPO_ROOT_BASE_ENV]!,
        identity,
      });
    }

    return { projectId, cycleInstanceId };
  }

  it("INS09/INS13/INS16/INS18 — G-D4 required facts from W2; confirm refuses before attest; inspect writes only on attest", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId } = await seedActiveCycle("a");
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const prep = await prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId,
      analysis: baseAnalysis(),
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
    });
    expect(prep.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(prep.message);

    const beforeHd = await oa.decisionServices.decisions.listByProject(projectId);
    const inspBefore =
      await oa.executionContractServices.inspectionAttestations.listByContract(
        prep.contract.executionContractId,
      );

    const continuity = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(continuity.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!continuity.ok || continuity.kind !== "active") {
      throw new Error("expected active");
    }
    expect(continuity.decisionRef).toBeNull();
    expect(continuity.requiredInspectedFacts).not.toBeNull();
    expect(continuity.requiredInspectedFacts!.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(continuity.inspection.inspectionSufficient).toBe(false);

    // Opening a view = presentation only (no write) — simulated by row build.
    const rows = buildInspectionFactRowsFromDisclosure({
      disclosure: continuity.contract.inspectionDisclosure,
      requiredInspectedFacts: continuity.requiredInspectedFacts!,
    });
    expect(rows.missingRequired).toEqual([]);
    const inspMid =
      await oa.executionContractServices.inspectionAttestations.listByContract(
        prep.contract.executionContractId,
      );
    expect(inspMid.length).toBe(inspBefore.length);

    const refused = await confirmExecutionContractForAuthorization({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
    });
    expect(refused.ok).toBe(false);

    const inspected = await inspectExecutionContract({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
      expectedVersion: prep.contract.version,
      inspectedFacts: rows.presentedFactIds,
    });
    expect(inspected.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!inspected.ok) throw new Error(inspected.message);
    expect(inspected.inspectionSufficient).toBe(true);

    const inspAfter =
      await oa.executionContractServices.inspectionAttestations.listByContract(
        prep.contract.executionContractId,
      );
    expect(inspAfter.length).toBe(inspBefore.length + 1);
    expect([...inspAfter[inspAfter.length - 1]!.inspectedFacts]).toEqual(
      [...rows.presentedFactIds],
    );

    const afterHd = await oa.decisionServices.decisions.listByProject(projectId);
    expect(afterHd.length).toBe(beforeHd.length);
    const attempts = await oa.executionAttemptServices!.listExecutionAttempts.execute(
      { executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId },
    );
    expect(attempts.ok).toBe(true);
    if (attempts.ok) expect(attempts.attempts).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("INS10 — incomplete attested facts → inspectionSufficient false", async () => {
    const { projectId, cycleInstanceId } = await seedActiveCycle("b");
    const oa = runtime.oa!;
    const prep = await prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft({
      oa,
      projectId,
      cycleInstanceId,
      analysis: baseAnalysis(),
      forceLocalAuthority: true,
      pinnedBaseHeadSha: W2_TEST_PINNED_BASE_HEAD_SHA,
    });
    expect(prep.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!prep.ok) throw new Error(prep.message);

    const continuity = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    if (!continuity.ok || continuity.kind !== "active") {
      throw new Error("expected active");
    }
    expect(continuity.requiredInspectedFacts).not.toBeNull();

    const inspected = await inspectExecutionContract({
      oa,
      projectId,
      executionContractId: prep.contract.executionContractId,
      expectedVersion: prep.contract.version,
      inspectedFacts: ["action", "scope"],
    });
    expect(inspected.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!inspected.ok) throw new Error(inspected.message);
    expect(inspected.inspectionSufficient).toBe(false);

    const after = await readCurrentGovernedExecutionContinuity({
      oa,
      projectId,
    });
    expect(after.ok).toBe(true);
    if (!after.ok || after.kind !== "active") throw new Error("expected active");
    expect(after.inspection.inspectionSufficient).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```
/* Pass04 — shared inspection exam panel (presentation only). */

.panel {
  display: flex;
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 10px;
  min-width: 0;
}

.title {
  margin: 0;
  font-size: 0.875rem;

}
```

```
"use client";

/**
 * Pass04 — shared Pilot exam panel for required contract facts.
 *
 * Presentation only: renders canonical disclosure rows and an explicit Attest CTA.
 * Does not compute required facts, persist, or grant authority.
 * Consumers: ExecutionSurface, GovernedConfirmationCard, TrajectorySurface.
 */

import type { InspectionFactRow } from "./pilotExecutionPresentation";
import type { InspectionExamSnapshot } from "./contractInspectionExam";
import styles from "./ContractInspectionExamPanel.module.css";

export type ContractInspectionExamPanelProps = {
  /** Prefix for data-testid (e.g. project-execution, governed-confirmation, w2-trajectory). */
  readonly testIdPrefix: string;
  readonly snapshot: InspectionExamSnapshot;
  readonly rows: readonly InspectionFactRow[];
  readonly missingRequired: readonly string[];
  readonly presentedFactIds: readonly string[];
  readonly busy: boolean;
  readonly onAttest: () => void;
  readonly onDismiss?: () => void;
};

export function ContractInspectionExamPanel({
  testIdPrefix,
  snapshot,
  rows,
  missingRequired,
  presentedFactIds,
  busy,
  onAttest,
  onDismiss,
}: ContractInspectionExamPanelProps) {
  const canAttest =
    missingRequired.length === 0 && presentedFactIds.length > 0 && !busy;

  return (
    <section
      className={styles.panel}
      aria-labelledby={`${testIdPrefix}-inspection-title`}
      data-testid={`${testIdPrefix}-inspection-panel`}
      data-exam-project={snapshot.projectId}
      data-exam-contract={snapshot.executionContractId}
      data-exam-version={String(snapshot.version)}
      data-exam-fingerprint={snapshot.semanticFingerprint}
    >
      <h3
        className={styles.title}
        id={`${testIdPrefix}-inspection-title`}
      >
        Faits contractuels à examiner
      </h3>
      <p className={styles.note}>
        Ces faits proviennent de la disclosure durable. L’ouverture de ce
        panneau n’enregistre aucune attestation.
      </p>
      <p
        className={styles.identity}
        data-testid={`${testIdPrefix}-inspection-identity`}
      >
        Contrat {snapshot.executionContractId} · v{snapshot.version}
      </p>
      {missingRequired.length > 0 ? (
        <p
          className={styles.missing}
          data-testid={`${testIdPrefix}-inspection-missing`}
          role="alert"
        >
          Faits obligatoires non restituables : {missingRequired.join(", ")}
        </p>
      ) : null}
      <ul className={styles.list}>
        {rows.map((row) => (
          <li
            key={row.factId}
            className={styles.item}
            data-testid={`${testIdPrefix}-inspection-fact-${row.factId}`}
            data-fact-id={row.factId}
          >
            <span className={styles.label}>{row.label}</span>
            <span className={styles.value}>{row.value}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <div className={styles.actions}>
        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.primary}
          data-testid={`${testIdPrefix}-attest`}
          disabled={!canAttest}
          aria-label="Attester l’examen des faits affichés"
          title="Atteste uniquement les faits affichés ci-dessus pour cette version."
          onClick={onAttest}
        >
          {busy ? "Attestation…" : "Attester l’examen"}
        </button>
        {onDismiss ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.secondary}
            data-testid={`${testIdPrefix}-inspection-dismiss`}
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={onDismiss}
          >
            Fermer
          </button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```
"use client";

/**
 * M-HD-02 — Generic Mission Pilot decision (INLINE in Conversation).
 * Pure projection of a sealed GenericMissionSubject + OA option ids.
 * Does NOT invent materiality, options, or HumanDecision.
 * Reuses GovernedDecisionCard visual language (Figma 412:2 / 414:2).
 */

import {
  UI_GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_ACCEPT_PREPARE,
  UI_GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REFUSE,
  UI_GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REQUEST_AMEND,
  type GenericMissionSubjectView,
} from "./genericMissionPresentation";
import styles from "./GovernedDecisionCard.module.css";

export type GenericMissionDecisionUiState =
  | "awaiting_decision"
  | "recording"
  | "accepted"
  | "refused"
  | "amended"
  | "stale"
  | "authority_denied"
  | "error";

export type GovernedGenericMissionDecisionCardProps = {
  readonly subject: GenericMissionSubjectView | null;
  readonly uiState: GenericMissionDecisionUiState;
  readonly busy: boolean;
  readonly error: string | null;
  readonly staleMessage?: string | null;
  readonly onAccept: () => void;
  readonly onRefuse: () => void;
  readonly onRequestAmend: () => void;
  /** Optional — open contract / detail disclosure (navigation only). */
  readonly onOpenDetail?: () => void;
};

function effectsSummary(subject: GenericMissionSubjectView): string {
  const caps = subject.effects.requiredCapabilities;
  const op = subject.effects.requestedOperation?.trim();
  const parts: string[] = [];
  if (op) parts.push(`Opération : ${op}`);
  if (caps.length > 0) parts.push(`Capacités : ${caps.join(", ")}`);
  if (subject.effects.expectedOutputs.length > 0) {
    parts.push(
      `Résultats : ${subject.effects.expectedOutputs.slice(0, 3).join("; ")}`,
    );
  }
  return parts.join(" · ") || "Effets matériels scellés avec la mission.";
}

function risksSummary(subject: GenericMissionSubjectView): string {
  const risks = subject.effects.risks.filter((r) => r.trim().length > 0);
  const reservations = subject.effects.reservations.filter(
    (r) => r.trim().length > 0,
  );
  const bits = [...risks.slice(0, 3), ...reservations.slice(0, 2)];
  return bits.length > 0
    ? bits.join(" · ")
    : "Aucune réserve supplémentaire scellée.";
}

export function GovernedGenericMissionDecisionCard({
  subject,
  uiState,
  busy,
  error,
  staleMessage,
  onAccept,
  onRefuse,
  onRequestAmend,
  onOpenDetail,
}: GovernedGenericMissionDecisionCardProps) {
  if (uiState === "stale" || (!subject && uiState !== "awaiting_decision")) {
    return (
      <section
        className={styles.card}
        data-testid="generic-mission-decision-card"
        data-state="stale"
        aria-labelledby="generic-mission-decision-title"
      >
        <p className={styles.label}>Mission générique</p>
        <h3
          id="generic-mission-decision-title"
          className={styles.title}
          data-testid="generic-mission-decision-title"
        >
          Présentation périmée
        </h3>
        <p className={styles.optionBody} data-testid="generic-mission-stale-body">
          {staleMessage?.trim() ||
            "Cette présentation de mission n'est plus actuelle. Demandez à Nora une nouvelle qualification avant toute décision."}
        </p>
        <p className={styles.optionMeta}>
          Aucune préparation n'est ouverte sur cette ancienne présentation.
        </p>
      </section>
    );
  }

  if (!subject) return null;

  const decided =
    uiState === "accepted" ||
    uiState === "refused" ||
    uiState === "amended";
  const actionsDisabled = busy || decided || uiState === "authority_denied";

  const structural =
    subject.materiality === "structural" ||
    subject.impactSignals.structuralChange ||
    subject.impactSignals.architectureImpact;

  const statusLine =
    uiState === "accepted"
      ? "Décision acceptée — la préparation peut reprendre sur cette mission exacte (préparer ≠ confirmer ≠ exécuter)."
      : uiState === "refused"
        ? "Décision refusée — aucune préparation débloquée."
        : uiState === "amended"
          ? "Modification demandée — nouvelle qualification et présentation requises."
          : uiState === "recording"
            ? "Enregistrement de la décision…"
            : uiState === "authority_denied"
              ? "Autorité insuffisante — décision non enregistrée."
              : "Décision explicite requise avant toute préparation.";

  return (
    <section
      className={styles.card}
      data-testid="generic-mission-decision-card"
      data-state={uiState}
      data-mission-subject-id={subject.missionSubjectId}
      data-presentation-digest={subject.presentationDigest}
      aria-labelledby="generic-mission-decision-title"
    >
      <p className={styles.label}>Mission générique</p>
      <h3
        id="generic-mission-decision-title"
        className={styles.title}
        data-testid="generic-mission-decision-title"
      >
        {subject.objective}
      </h3>
      <p className={styles.youDecide} data-testid="generic-mission-status">
        {statusLine}
      </p>

      <div className={styles.optionBlock} data-testid="generic-mission-outcome">
        <p className={styles.optionEyebrow}>Résultat attendu</p>
        <p className={styles.optionBody}>{subject.expectedOutcome}</p>
      </div>
      <div className={styles.optionBlock} data-testid="generic-mission-scope">
        <p className={styles.optionEyebrow}>Périmètre</p>
        <p className={styles.optionBody}>{subject.scopeNarrative}</p>
        {subject.scopeIn.length > 0 ? (
          <p className={styles.optionMeta}>
            Inclus : {subject.scopeIn.slice(0, 4).join(", ")}
            {subject.scopeOut.length > 0
              ? ` · Hors : ${subject.scopeOut.slice(0, 3).join(", ")}`
              : ""}
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      <div className={styles.optionBlock} data-testid="generic-mission-effects">
        <p className={styles.optionEyebrow}>Effets matériels</p>
        <p className={styles.optionBody}>{effectsSummary(subject)}</p>
        {structural ? (
          <p className={styles.optionMeta} data-testid="generic-mission-structural">
            Mission structurante — HumanDecision Pilote requise.
          </p>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      <div className={styles.optionBlock} data-testid="generic-mission-risks">
        <p className={styles.optionEyebrow}>Risques et réserves</p>
        <p className={styles.optionBody}>{risksSummary(subject)}</p>
      </div>

      {error ? (
        <p
          className={styles.error}
          role="alert"
          data-testid="generic-mission-decision-error"
        >
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}

      {!decided ? (
        <div className={styles.actions}>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.primary}
            data-testid="generic-mission-accept"
            data-option-id={UI_GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_ACCEPT_PREPARE}
            disabled={actionsDisabled}
            onClick={onAccept}
          >
            {busy ? "Enregistrement…" : "Accepter la mission"}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.secondary}
            data-testid="generic-mission-refuse"
            data-option-id={UI_GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REFUSE}
            disabled={actionsDisabled}
            onClick={onRefuse}
          >
            Refuser
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            className={styles.tertiary}
            data-testid="generic-mission-request-amend"
            data-option-id={UI_GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REQUEST_AMEND}
            disabled={actionsDisabled}
            onClick={onRequestAmend}
          >
            Demander à modifier
          </button>
          {onOpenDetail ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              className={styles.tertiary}
              data-testid="generic-mission-open-detail"
              disabled={busy}
              onClick={onOpenDetail}
            >
              Voir le détail
            </button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </section>
  );
}
```

#### NEW FILE `projects/sfia-studio/app/features/pre-m6-product-ui/surfaces/contractInspectionExam.ts`

```
/**
 * Pass04 — unified inspection exam helpers (presentation only).
 *
 * Source of required facts: OA `requiredInspectedFactsForContract` via W2 DTO.
 * No persistence. No authority. No second required-facts calculator.
 */

import type { ExecutionContractInspectionDisclosure } from "@/lib/oa/execution-contract";
import {
  buildInspectionFactRowsFromDisclosure,
  type InspectionFactRow,
} from "./pilotExecutionPresentation";

/** Identity of the exam currently open — invalidate if any field changes. */
export type InspectionExamSnapshot = {
  readonly projectId: string;
  readonly executionContractId: string;
  readonly version: number;
  readonly semanticFingerprint: string;
};

export type InspectionExamView = {
  readonly rows: readonly InspectionFactRow[];
  readonly missingRequired: readonly string[];
  readonly presentedFactIds: readonly string[];
  readonly disclosureIncomplete: boolean;
};

export function sameInspectionExamSnapshot(
  a: InspectionExamSnapshot | null | undefined,
  b: InspectionExamSnapshot | null | undefined,
): boolean {
  if (!a || !b) return false;
  return (
    a.projectId === b.projectId &&
    a.executionContractId === b.executionContractId &&
    a.version === b.version &&
    a.semanticFingerprint === b.semanticFingerprint
  );
}

/**
 * Build display rows from canonical disclosure + required fact IDs.
 * Refuses (disclosureIncomplete) when required list is null/absent.
 */
export function buildInspectionExamView(input: {
  readonly disclosure: ExecutionContractInspectionDisclosure | null | undefined;
  readonly requiredInspectedFacts: readonly string[] | null | undefined;
}): InspectionExamView {
  if (
    input.disclosure == null ||
    input.requiredInspectedFacts == null ||
    !Array.isArray(input.requiredInspectedFacts)
  ) {
    return {
      rows: [],
      missingRequired: ["INSPECTION_DISCLOSURE_INCOMPLETE"],
      presentedFactIds: [],
      disclosureIncomplete: true,
    };
  }
  return {
    ...buildInspectionFactRowsFromDisclosure({
      disclosure: input.disclosure,
      requiredInspectedFacts: input.requiredInspectedFacts,
    }),
    disclosureIncomplete: false,
  };
}
```

```
/**
 * M-HD-02 — client-safe Generic Mission presentation types & option ids.
 * Option ids MUST match w2/genericMissionSubject.ts (server authority).
 * No OA / node imports — safe for ConversationSurface client bundle.
 */

export const UI_GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_ACCEPT_PREPARE =
  "opt:generic-mission:accept-prepare" as const;
export const UI_GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REFUSE =
  "opt:generic-mission:refuse" as const;
export const UI_GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REQUEST_AMEND =
  "opt:generic-mission:request-amend" as const;

/** Projection of sealed subject fields needed by the Pilot card. */
export type GenericMissionSubjectView = {
  readonly missionSubjectId: string;
  readonly presentationDigest: string;
  readonly projectId: string;
  readonly cycleInstanceId: string | null;
  readonly objective: string;
  readonly expectedOutcome: string;
  readonly scopeNarrative: string;
  readonly scopeIn: readonly string[];
  readonly scopeOut: readonly string[];
  readonly materiality: "structural" | "non_structural" | "unknown";
  readonly impactSignals: {
    readonly structuralChange: boolean;
    readonly securityImpact: boolean;
    readonly architectureImpact: boolean;
    readonly dataImpact: boolean;
    readonly irreversible: boolean;
  };
  readonly effects: {
    readonly intentKind: string | null;
    readonly artifactType: string | null;
    readonly requestedOperation: string | null;
    readonly requiredCapabilities: readonly string[];
    readonly expectedOutputs: readonly string[];
    readonly risks: readonly string[];
    readonly reservations: readonly string[];
  };
};
```

```
/**
 * M-HD-02 — applicable durable HumanDecision vs CURRENT sealed presentation.
 * No seal / offer / UI imports — safe for Record + Continuity.
 */

import type { HumanDecision } from "@/lib/oa/decision";
import {
  materialCurrentnessMatch,
  verifyGenericMissionSnapshotIntegrity,
  type GenericMissionCurrentMaterial,
  type GenericMissionSubject,
  type LpsMaterialSnapshot,
} from "./genericMissionSubject";

/** Map OA LPS current read → material snapshot. Never invent from sealed subject. */
export function lpsMaterialSnapshotFromLivingProjectState(lps: {
  readonly version: number;
  readonly objective: string;
  readonly context?: string;
  readonly scope?: string;
  readonly constraints?: readonly string[];
  readonly doctrinePackageRef?: unknown;
  readonly activeCycleInstanceId?: string | null;
  readonly trajectoryId?: string | null;
  readonly trajectoryVersion?: number | null;
  readonly decisionIds?: readonly string[];
}): LpsMaterialSnapshot {
  const ref = lps.doctrinePackageRef as
    | { digest?: string; contentDigest?: string; packageDigest?: string }
    | null
    | undefined;
  return {
    version: lps.version,
    objective: lps.objective,
    context: lps.context,
    scope: lps.scope,
    constraints: lps.constraints ? [...lps.constraints] : undefined,
    doctrineDigest:
      (typeof ref?.digest === "string" ? ref.digest.trim() : "") ||
      (typeof ref?.contentDigest === "string" ? ref.contentDigest.trim() : "") ||
      (typeof ref?.packageDigest === "string" ? ref.packageDigest.trim() : "") ||
      "",
    activeCycleInstanceId: lps.activeCycleInstanceId ?? null,
    trajectoryId: lps.trajectoryId ?? null,
    trajectoryVersion: lps.trajectoryVersion ?? null,
    decisionIds: lps.decisionIds ? [...lps.decisionIds] : undefined,
  };
}

const INAPPLICABLE_STATUSES = new Set<HumanDecision["status"]>([
  "superseded",
  "revoked",
  "proposed",
  "required",
]);

function currentMaterialFromSubject(
  subject: GenericMissionSubject,
): GenericMissionCurrentMaterial {
  return {
    objective: subject.objective,
    expectedOutcome: subject.expectedOutcome,
    scopeNarrative: subject.scopeNarrative,
    scopeIn: subject.scopeIn,
    scopeOut: subject.scopeOut,
    materiality: subject.materiality,
    repositoryBindingIdentity: subject.repositoryBindingIdentity,
    pathRoot: subject.pathRoot,
    requiredCapabilities: subject.effects.requiredCapabilities,
    expectedOutputs: subject.effects.expectedOutputs,
    intentKind: subject.effects.intentKind,
    artifactType: subject.effects.artifactType,
    requestedOperation: subject.effects.requestedOperation,
    reversibilityExpectation: subject.effects.reversibilityExpectation,
    stopConditions: subject.effects.stopConditions,
    risks: subject.effects.risks,
    reservations: subject.effects.reservations,
    impactSignals: subject.impactSignals,
    cycleInstanceId: subject.cycleInstanceId,
  };
}

/**
 * Decide whether a durable HD is currently applicable to the sealed presentation.
 * Historical ≠ applicable. Digest / material mismatch → inapplicable.
 */
export function assessApplicableGenericMissionDecision(input: {
  readonly decision: HumanDecision;
  readonly subject: GenericMissionSubject;
  readonly currentLps?: LpsMaterialSnapshot | null;
}):
  | { readonly applicable: true; readonly decision: HumanDecision }
  | { readonly applicable: false; readonly reason: string } {
  const d = input.decision;
  if (d.projectId !== input.subject.projectId) {
    return { applicable: false, reason: "project_mismatch" };
  }
  if (d.subject !== input.subject.missionSubjectId) {
    return { applicable: false, reason: "subject_mismatch" };
  }
  if (INAPPLICABLE_STATUSES.has(d.status)) {
    return { applicable: false, reason: `status_${d.status}` };
  }
  if (
    d.status !== "accepted" &&
    d.status !== "refused" &&
    d.status !== "amended"
  ) {
    return { applicable: false, reason: `status_${d.status}` };
  }
  const basis = d.decisionBasis;
  if (!basis) {
    return { applicable: false, reason: "decision_basis_absent" };
  }
  const integrity = verifyGenericMissionSnapshotIntegrity({ basis });
  if (!integrity.ok) {
    return { applicable: false, reason: integrity.reason };
  }
  if (basis.sourceType !== "generic_mission") {
    return { applicable: false, reason: "source_type_not_generic_mission" };
  }
  const ctx = basis.genericMissionContext;
  if (!ctx) {
    return { applicable: false, reason: "generic_mission_context_absent" };
  }
  if (ctx.missionSubjectId !== input.subject.missionSubjectId) {
    return { applicable: false, reason: "mission_subject_id_mismatch" };
  }
  const material = materialCurrentnessMatch({
    sealed: ctx,
    current: currentMaterialFromSubject(input.subject),
  });
  if (!material.ok) {
    return { applicable: false, reason: material.reason };
  }

  const digestMatches =
    ctx.presentationDigest === input.subject.presentationDigest;
  const sealedVersion = basis.proposalContext.lpsVersion;
  const currentVersion = input.currentLps?.version ?? input.subject.lpsVersion;

  // Digest may diverge solely from LPS anchors after a linked Record HD bump.
  // Material effects already matched — require linked LPS when digest differs.
  if (!digestMatches) {
    if (d.status !== "accepted") {
      return { applicable: false, reason: "presentation_digest_mismatch" };
    }
    if (currentVersion <= sealedVersion) {
      return { applicable: false, reason: "presentation_digest_mismatch" };
    }
    const ids = input.currentLps?.decisionIds ?? [];
    if (!ids.includes(d.decisionId)) {
      return { applicable: false, reason: "lps_material_bump_unlinked" };
    }
  }

  // LPS: accepted HD may legitimately bump version when decisionId is linked.
  // Unlinked material bump / doctrine drift → inapplicable (not a silent GO).
  if (d.status === "accepted" && input.currentLps) {
    if (input.currentLps.version < sealedVersion) {
      return { applicable: false, reason: "lps_version_regressed" };
    }
    if (input.currentLps.version > sealedVersion) {
      const ids = input.currentLps.decisionIds ?? [];
      if (!ids.includes(d.decisionId)) {
        return { applicable: false, reason: "lps_material_bump_unlinked" };
      }
      const sealedDoctrine = basis.proposalContext.doctrineDigest ?? "";
      if (
        sealedDoctrine &&
        sealedDoctrine !== (input.currentLps.doctrineDigest ?? "")
      ) {
        return { applicable: false, reason: "lps_doctrine_drift" };
      }
      const sealedCycle =
        basis.proposalContext.activeCycleInstanceId ??
        ctx.cycleInstanceId ??
        null;
      if (
        (sealedCycle ?? null) !==
        (input.currentLps.activeCycleInstanceId ?? null)
      ) {
        return { applicable: false, reason: "lps_cycle_drift" };
      }
    }
  }

  return { applicable: true, decision: d };
}

/**
 * Select the single applicable durable HD for the CURRENT sealed presentation.
 * Ambiguous applicable set → fail-closed.
 */
export function selectApplicableGenericMissionDecision(input: {
  readonly decisions: readonly HumanDecision[];
  readonly subject: GenericMissionSubject;
  readonly currentLps?: LpsMaterialSnapshot | null;
}):
  | { readonly ok: true; readonly decision: HumanDecision | null }
  | { readonly ok: false; readonly code: string; readonly message: string } {
  const applicable: HumanDecision[] = [];
  for (const d of input.decisions) {
    const assessed = assessApplicableGenericMissionDecision({
      decision: d,
      subject: input.subject,
      currentLps: input.currentLps,
    });
    if (assessed.applicable) applicable.push(assessed.decision);
  }
  if (applicable.length === 0) {
    return { ok: true, decision: null };
  }
  if (applicable.length === 1) {
    return { ok: true, decision: applicable[0]! };
  }
  // Prefer most recent effectiveAt among identical status/option — still fail if mixed.
  const statuses = new Set(applicable.map((d) => d.status));
  const options = new Set(applicable.map((d) => d.selectedOptionId));
  if (statuses.size > 1 || options.size > 1) {
    return {
      ok: false,
      code: "OA_DECISION_HISTORY_AMBIGUOUS",
      message:
        "Plusieurs HumanDecisions applicables ambiguës — nouvelle présentation requise.",
    };
  }
  const latest = applicable.reduce((a, b) =>
    (a.effectiveAt ?? "") >= (b.effectiveAt ?? "") ? a : b,
  );
  return { ok: true, decision: latest };
}
```
