```
/**
 * M-HD-02 — read Generic Mission presentation continuity for Pilot UI.
 *
 * Source = CURRENT pending prepare offer (process-local) → Product seal.
 * No PresentedMission store. Restart without offer → none / stale (fail closed).
 * Decision truth = durable OA HumanDecision only — applicable only when
 * DecisionBasis / presentationDigest match the CURRENT sealed presentation.
 */

import type { HumanDecision } from "@/lib/oa/decision";
import type { RuntimeOaStack } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime";
import {
  analysisFromPendingGenericPrepareOffer,
  assessGenericMissionPrepareReadiness,
  getCurrentGenericPrepareOffer,
} from "./prepareGenericExecutionContractDraft";
import { resolveProductPrepareOfferSessionKey } from "./pendingGenericPrepareOfferStore";
import {
  GENERIC_MISSION_PRESENTED_OPTIONS,
  evaluateLpsTransitionAfterHumanDecision,
  type GenericMissionSubject,
} from "./genericMissionSubject";
import {
  lpsMaterialSnapshotFromLivingProjectState,
  selectApplicableGenericMissionDecision,
} from "./genericMissionDecisionApplicability";
import { sealGenericMissionForPresentation } from "./recordGenericMissionHumanDecision";

export { lpsMaterialSnapshotFromLivingProjectState } from "./genericMissionDecisionApplicability";

export type GenericMissionPresentedOption = {
  readonly optionId: string;
  readonly label: string;
};

export type GenericMissionDecisionContinuityResult =
  | {
      readonly ok: true;
      readonly kind: "awaiting_decision";
      readonly subject: GenericMissionSubject;
      readonly options: readonly GenericMissionPresentedOption[];
      readonly offerFingerprint: string;
      readonly sessionKey: string;
      readonly humanDecisionCreated: false;
      readonly executionAttemptCreated: false;
    }
  | {
      readonly ok: true;
      readonly kind: "decided";
      readonly subject: GenericMissionSubject;
      readonly decision: {
        readonly decisionId: string;
        readonly status: HumanDecision["status"];
        readonly selectedOptionId: string;
        readonly actorId: string;
        readonly presentationDigest: string;
      };
      readonly options: readonly GenericMissionPresentedOption[];
      readonly offerFingerprint: string;
      readonly sessionKey: string;
      readonly humanDecisionCreated: false;
      readonly executionAttemptCreated: false;
    }
  | {
      readonly ok: true;
      readonly kind: "none";
      readonly message: string;
      readonly humanDecisionCreated: false;
      readonly executionAttemptCreated: false;
    }
  | {
      readonly ok: true;
      readonly kind: "stale_presentation_required";
      readonly message: string;
      readonly humanDecisionCreated: false;
      readonly executionAttemptCreated: false;
    }
  | {
      readonly ok: false;
      readonly code: string;
      readonly message: string;
      readonly humanDecisionCreated: false;
      readonly executionAttemptCreated: false;
    };

/** Stable occurrence id from CURRENT offer — avoids silent re-occurrence on reread. */
export function missionSubjectIdFromOfferFingerprint(
  offerFingerprint: string,
): string {
  const fp = offerFingerprint.trim();
  const slice = fp.length > 40 ? fp.slice(0, 40) : fp;
  return `gms:offer:${slice}`;
}

function presentedOptions(): readonly GenericMissionPresentedOption[] {
  return GENERIC_MISSION_PRESENTED_OPTIONS.map((o) => ({
    optionId: o.optionId,
    label: o.label,
  }));
}

export {
  assessApplicableGenericMissionDecision,
  selectApplicableGenericMissionDecision,
} from "./genericMissionDecisionApplicability";

/**
 * Seal CURRENT pending offer for Pilot presentation when the mission is
 * structural / HD-required. Non-structural offers stay on chat-first PREPARE.
 */
export async function readGenericMissionDecisionContinuity(input: {
  readonly oa: RuntimeOaStack;
  readonly projectId: string;
  readonly sessionKey?: string | null;
  readonly sessionDbPath?: string | null;
  /**
   * When client held a presentationDigest but server has no reconstitutable
   * offer — declare stale (Figma 414:2).
   */
  readonly clientPresentationDigest?: string | null;
}): Promise<GenericMissionDecisionContinuityResult> {
  const fail = (
    code: string,
    message: string,
  ): GenericMissionDecisionContinuityResult => ({
    ok: false,
    code,
    message,
    humanDecisionCreated: false,
    executionAttemptCreated: false,
  });

  const projectId = input.projectId.trim();
  if (!projectId) {
    return fail("PRODUCT_PROJECT_REQUIRED", "Project requis.");
  }

  const sessionKey = resolveProductPrepareOfferSessionKey({
    sessionKey: input.sessionKey,
    sessionDbPath: input.sessionDbPath,
  });

  const offer = getCurrentGenericPrepareOffer(projectId, {
    sessionKey,
  });

  if (!offer) {
    if (input.clientPresentationDigest?.trim()) {
      return {
        ok: true,
        kind: "stale_presentation_required",
        message:
          "Cette présentation de mission n'est plus actuelle. Demandez à Nora une nouvelle qualification avant toute décision.",
        humanDecisionCreated: false,
        executionAttemptCreated: false,
      };
    }
    return {
      ok: true,
      kind: "none",
      message: "Aucune mission générique présentée pour ce projet.",
      humanDecisionCreated: false,
      executionAttemptCreated: false,
    };
  }

  if (offer.projectId !== projectId) {
    return fail(
      "MISSION_OFFER_PROJECT_MISMATCH",
      "Offre Product hors Project demandé — lecture refusée.",
    );
  }
  if (offer.sessionKey !== sessionKey) {
    return fail(
      "MISSION_OFFER_SESSION_MISMATCH",
      "Offre Product hors session serveur — lecture refusée.",
    );
  }

  const analysis = analysisFromPendingGenericPrepareOffer(offer);
  const readiness = assessGenericMissionPrepareReadiness(analysis);
  const structural =
    readiness.structuralBlocked ||
    offer.signalStructuralChange ||
    offer.signalArchitectureImpact ||
    offer.signalSecurityImpact ||
    offer.signalIrreversible;

  if (!structural) {
    return {
      ok: true,
      kind: "none",
      message:
        "Offre courante non structurante — parcours chat-first PREPARE inchangé.",
      humanDecisionCreated: false,
      executionAttemptCreated: false,
    };
  }

  if (readiness.missingFields.length > 0) {
    return {
      ok: true,
      kind: "stale_presentation_required",
      message:
        "Mission insuffisamment qualifiée pour décision — nouvelle présentation requise.",
      humanDecisionCreated: false,
      executionAttemptCreated: false,
    };
  }

  const missionSubjectId = missionSubjectIdFromOfferFingerprint(
    offer.offerFingerprint,
  );
  const sealed = await sealGenericMissionForPresentation({
    oa: input.oa,
    projectId,
    cycleInstanceId: offer.cycleInstanceId,
    analysis,
    missionSubjectId,
  });
  if (!sealed.ok) {
    return fail(sealed.code, sealed.message);
  }

  const clientDigest = input.clientPresentationDigest?.trim() || "";
  const sealedDigest = sealed.subject.presentationDigest;
  const digestMismatch = Boolean(clientDigest && clientDigest !== sealedDigest);

  // Post-Record path: OA history + LPS currentness BEFORE client-digest stale.
  // A legitimate HD→LPS bump changes the sealed digest (D0→D1) while the Pilote
  // UI still holds D0 — that must not become stale_presentation_required.
  const history = await input.oa.decisionServices.listDecisionHistory.execute({
    projectId,
    subject: sealed.subject.missionSubjectId,
  });
  if (!history.ok) {
    return fail(
      "OA_DECISION_HISTORY_UNREADABLE",
      "Historique OA illisible — aucune décision présentée comme applicable.",
    );
  }

  const lpsNow = await input.oa.projectServices.getCurrentLivingProjectState.execute({
    projectId,
  });
  // C — LPS fail-closed: never invent a "current" snapshot from the sealed subject.
  if (!lpsNow.ok) {
    return fail(
      "LPS_CURRENT_UNREADABLE",
      "État Living Project courant illisible — aucune décision présentée comme applicable.",
    );
  }
  const currentLps = lpsMaterialSnapshotFromLivingProjectState(
    lpsNow.livingProjectState,
  );

  const selected = selectApplicableGenericMissionDecision({
    decisions: history.decisions,
    subject: sealed.subject,
    currentLps,
  });
  if (!selected.ok) {
    return fail(selected.code, selected.message);
  }

  if (selected.decision) {
    const d = selected.decision;
    const ctx = d.decisionBasis?.genericMissionContext;
    const sealedLpsVersion = d.decisionBasis?.proposalContext.lpsVersion;

    // Material LPS currentness (existing CR-04 policy) when version advanced.
    if (
      d.status === "accepted" &&
      typeof sealedLpsVersion === "number" &&
      currentLps.version > sealedLpsVersion
    ) {
      const sealedLps = await input.oa.projectServices.getLivingProjectStateVersion.execute(
        {
          projectId,
          version: sealedLpsVersion,
        },
      );
      if (!sealedLps.ok) {
        return fail(
          "LPS_SEALED_UNREADABLE",
          "LPS scellé illisible — transition non qualifiable; aucune décision présentée comme applicable.",
        );
      }
      const sealedSnap = lpsMaterialSnapshotFromLivingProjectState(
        sealedLps.livingProjectState,
      );
      const sealedWithDoctrine = {
        ...sealedSnap,
        doctrineDigest:
          sealedSnap.doctrineDigest ||
          d.decisionBasis?.proposalContext.doctrineDigest?.trim() ||
          "",
      };
      const lpsEval = evaluateLpsTransitionAfterHumanDecision({
        sealed: sealedWithDoctrine,
        current: currentLps,
        decisionId: d.decisionId,
      });
      if (!lpsEval.ok) {
        // Case B — material / unlinked LPS drift: not a current GO.
        if (digestMismatch) {
          return {
            ok: true,
            kind: "stale_presentation_required",
            message:
              "Cette présentation de mission n'est plus actuelle. Demandez à Nora une nouvelle qualification avant toute décision.",
            humanDecisionCreated: false,
            executionAttemptCreated: false,
          };
        }
        return {
          ok: true,
          kind: "awaiting_decision",
          subject: sealed.subject,
          options: presentedOptions(),
          offerFingerprint: offer.offerFingerprint,
          sessionKey,
          humanDecisionCreated: false,
          executionAttemptCreated: false,
        };
      }
    }

    // Case A — applicable durable HD (incl. after legitimate LPS bump / D0 client digest).
    // When UI held D0, prefer returning the historically presented digest on the decision.
    return {
      ok: true,
      kind: "decided",
      subject: sealed.subject,
      decision: {
        decisionId: d.decisionId,
        status: d.status,
        selectedOptionId: d.selectedOptionId,
        actorId: d.actor.actorId,
        presentationDigest:
          ctx?.presentationDigest ?? sealed.subject.presentationDigest,
      },
      options: presentedOptions(),
      offerFingerprint: offer.offerFingerprint,
      sessionKey,
      humanDecisionCreated: false,
      executionAttemptCreated: false,
    };
  }

  // No applicable durable HD — client digest divergence means stale presentation.
  if (digestMismatch) {
    return {
      ok: true,
      kind: "stale_presentation_required",
      message:
        "Cette présentation de mission n'est plus actuelle. Demandez à Nora une nouvelle qualification avant toute décision.",
      humanDecisionCreated: false,
      executionAttemptCreated: false,
    };
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    kind: "awaiting_decision",
    subject: sealed.subject,
    options: presentedOptions(),
    offerFingerprint: offer.offerFingerprint,
    sessionKey,
    humanDecisionCreated: false,
    executionAttemptCreated: false,
  };
}
```

```
/**
 * M-HD-01 — present + record Pilote HumanDecision on a sealed Generic Mission.
 *
 * PREPARE / AUTHORIZE / EXECUTE are never performed here.
 * No Attempt. No fabricated decision. Refuse ≠ GO. Amend requires new seal.
 */

import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import {
  LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR,
  registerLocalPiloteAuthority,
  type HumanDecision,
  type OaActorReference,
} from "@/lib/oa/decision";
import type { RuntimeOaStack } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime";
import { resolveAuthoritativeProductMateriality } from "./resolveAuthoritativeProductMateriality";
import { resolveGenericPrepareCapabilityEnvelope } from "./genericPrepareCapabilityEnvelope";
import type { IntentAnalysisDto } from "../f2/types";
import {
  GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_ACCEPT_PREPARE,
  GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REFUSE,
  GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REQUEST_AMEND,
  GENERIC_MISSION_PRESENTED_OPTIONS,
  buildGenericMissionSubject,
  decisionBasisFromGenericMissionSubject,
  type GenericMissionSubject,
} from "./genericMissionSubject";
import {
  assessApplicableGenericMissionDecision,
  lpsMaterialSnapshotFromLivingProjectState,
} from "./genericMissionDecisionApplicability";
import type { LpsMaterialSnapshot } from "./genericMissionSubject";

/**
 * Authenticated Pilote for Product Record — actor must come from
 * resolveCurrentAuthenticatedPilote (server session). Client claims ignored.
 */
export type AuthenticatedPiloteForMissionDecision = {
  readonly actor: OaActorReference;
  readonly githubUserId?: string;
};

export type SealGenericMissionForPresentationInput = {
  readonly oa: RuntimeOaStack;
  readonly projectId: string;
  readonly cycleInstanceId?: string | null;
  readonly analysis: IntentAnalysisDto;
  /** Reuse existing occurrence id on amend re-presentation only when material unchanged. */
  readonly missionSubjectId?: string;
};

export type SealGenericMissionForPresentationResult =
  | {
      readonly ok: true;
      readonly subject: GenericMissionSubject;
      readonly humanDecisionCreated: false;
      readonly executionAttemptCreated: false;
    }
  | {
      readonly ok: false;
      readonly code: string;
      readonly message: string;
      readonly humanDecisionCreated: false;
      readonly executionAttemptCreated: false;
    };

export async function sealGenericMissionForPresentation(
  input: SealGenericMissionForPresentationInput,
): Promise<SealGenericMissionForPresentationResult> {
  const fail = (
    code: string,
    message: string,
  ): SealGenericMissionForPresentationResult => ({
    ok: false,
    code,
    message,
    humanDecisionCreated: false,
    executionAttemptCreated: false,
  });

  const projectId = input.projectId.trim();
  if (!projectId) {
    return fail("PRODUCT_PROJECT_REQUIRED", "Project requis.");
  }

  const materialityResolution = await resolveAuthoritativeProductMateriality({
    oa: input.oa,
    projectId,
    analysis: input.analysis,
    cycleInstanceId: input.cycleInstanceId,
  });
  if (!materialityResolution.liveReadOk) {
    return fail(
      materialityResolution.failureCode ?? "PRODUCT_CONTEXT_UNAVAILABLE",
      "Contexte Product insuffisant pour sceller la mission.",
    );
  }
  const currentness = materialityResolution.facts.productCurrentness;
  if (!currentness) {
    return fail(
      "PRODUCT_CURRENTNESS_UNAVAILABLE",
      "Ancres LPS / doctrine indisponibles pour le sceau.",
    );
  }

  const proposedCapabilities =
    input.analysis.executionIntent?.requiredCapabilities ?? [];
  const capabilityResolution =
    resolveGenericPrepareCapabilityEnvelope(proposedCapabilities);
  if (!capabilityResolution.ok) {
    return fail(
      capabilityResolution.code,
      `Capacités hors enveloppe — sceau refusé : ${capabilityResolution.elevated.join(", ")}.`,
    );
  }

  const objective = (
    input.analysis.objective ??
    input.analysis.rephrasedRequest ??
    ""
  ).trim();
  const expectedOutcome = (input.analysis.expectedOutcome ?? objective).trim();
  const scopeNarrative = (input.analysis.scope ?? expectedOutcome).trim();
  if (!objective || (!expectedOutcome && !scopeNarrative)) {
    return fail(
      "MISSION_INSUFFICIENTLY_QUALIFIED",
      "Objectif / résultat attendu insuffisants pour présentation Pilote.",
    );
  }

  // Nora path/filename candidates stay non-authoritative — never sealed as destinations.
  const ei = input.analysis.executionIntent;
  const authoritativeExpectedOutputs = [expectedOutcome];
  const requestedOperation =
    ei && "requestedOperation" in ei && typeof ei.requestedOperation === "string"
      ? ei.requestedOperation.trim() || null
      : input.analysis.requestedOperation?.trim() || null;

  const signals = input.analysis.signals;
  const revFromEi =
    ei &&
    "reversibilityExpectation" in ei &&
    (ei.reversibilityExpectation === "reversible" ||
      ei.reversibilityExpectation === "irreversible" ||
      ei.reversibilityExpectation === "unknown")
      ? ei.reversibilityExpectation
      : null;
  const subject = buildGenericMissionSubject({
    missionSubjectId: input.missionSubjectId,
    projectId,
    cycleInstanceId: input.cycleInstanceId?.trim() || null,
    objective,
    expectedOutcome,
    scopeNarrative,
    scopeIn: ei?.scopeIn ?? [],
    scopeOut: input.analysis.outOfScope ?? [],
    materiality: materialityResolution.assessment.materiality,
    materialityReasonCodes: materialityResolution.assessment.reasonCodes,
    repositoryBindingIdentity: currentness.repositoryIdentity,
    pathRoot: currentness.pathRoot,
    effects: {
      intentKind: ei?.intentKind?.trim() || null,
      artifactType: ei?.artifactType?.trim() || null,
      requestedOperation,
      requiredCapabilities: [...capabilityResolution.contractCapabilities],
      expectedOutputs: authoritativeExpectedOutputs,
      reversibilityExpectation:
        revFromEi ??
        (signals?.irreversible === true
          ? "irreversible"
          : signals?.irreversible === false
            ? "reversible"
            : "unknown"),
      stopConditions: [
        ...(input.analysis.stopConditions ?? []).filter((s) => s.trim()),
        "NO_EXECUTE_WITHOUT_EXPLICIT_AUTHORIZE",
      ],
      risks: [...(input.analysis.risks ?? [])],
      reservations: [...(input.analysis.reservations ?? [])],
    },
    impactSignals: {
      structuralChange: Boolean(signals?.structuralChange),
      securityImpact: Boolean(signals?.securityImpact),
      architectureImpact: Boolean(signals?.architectureImpact),
      dataImpact: Boolean(signals?.dataImpact),
      irreversible: Boolean(signals?.irreversible),
    },
    lpsId: currentness.lpsId,
    lpsVersion: currentness.lpsVersion,
    doctrineDigest: currentness.doctrineDigest,
    presentedAtIso: input.oa.clock.nowIso(),
  });

  return {
    ok: true,
    subject,
    humanDecisionCreated: false,
    executionAttemptCreated: false,
  };
}

export type RecordGenericMissionHumanDecisionInput = {
  readonly oa: RuntimeOaStack;
  readonly subject: GenericMissionSubject;
  readonly selectedOptionId: string;
  /**
   * CR-02 — server re-seals from this analysis + Product facts; caller subject
   * digest must match. Client-only subject is not authority.
   */
  readonly analysis: IntentAnalysisDto;
  /**
   * Product path — session-bound Pilote. Required unless forceLocalAuthority
   * (tests only). Never accept a client-forged ActorReference as authority.
   */
  readonly authenticatedPilote?: AuthenticatedPiloteForMissionDecision;
  /** Test inject only — never on real Product Record path (A7). */
  readonly forceLocalAuthority?: boolean;
  readonly rationale?: string;
  readonly supersedeExistingAccepted?: boolean;
  /** When true (default), link accepted HD into LPS (legitimate version bump). */
  readonly linkToLivingProjectState?: boolean;
  /**
   * Digest of the presentation the Pilote actually saw (client/UI).
   * When omitted, defaults to subject.presentationDigest.
   * Used to recognize normal LPS-bump replay without inventing a new HD.
   */
  readonly presentedPresentationDigest?: string;
};

export type RecordGenericMissionHumanDecisionResult =
  | {
      readonly ok: true;
      readonly decision: HumanDecision;
      readonly humanDecisionCreated: boolean;
      readonly executionAttemptCreated: false;
      readonly code:
        | "GENERIC_MISSION_HUMAN_DECISION_RECORDED"
        | "GENERIC_MISSION_HUMAN_DECISION_ALREADY_APPLIED";
    }
  | {
      readonly ok: false;
      readonly code: string;
      readonly message: string;
      readonly humanDecisionCreated: false;
      readonly executionAttemptCreated: false;
    };

export async function recordGenericMissionHumanDecision(
  input: RecordGenericMissionHumanDecisionInput,
): Promise<RecordGenericMissionHumanDecisionResult> {
  const fail = (
    code: string,
    message: string,
  ): RecordGenericMissionHumanDecisionResult => ({
    ok: false,
    code,
    message,
    humanDecisionCreated: false,
    executionAttemptCreated: false,
  });

  const selected = input.selectedOptionId.trim();
  const presentedIds = new Set(
    GENERIC_MISSION_PRESENTED_OPTIONS.map((o) => o.optionId),
  );
  if (!presentedIds.has(selected as (typeof GENERIC_MISSION_PRESENTED_OPTIONS)[number]["optionId"])) {
    return fail(
      "HD_OPTION_NOT_PRESENTED",
      "Option Pilote hors ensemble présenté — décision refusée.",
    );
  }

  // Self-consistency of caller subject (digest over its own fields).
  const selfCheck = buildGenericMissionSubject({
    missionSubjectId: input.subject.missionSubjectId,
    projectId: input.subject.projectId,
    cycleInstanceId: input.subject.cycleInstanceId,
    objective: input.subject.objective,
    expectedOutcome: input.subject.expectedOutcome,
    scopeNarrative: input.subject.scopeNarrative,
    scopeIn: input.subject.scopeIn,
    scopeOut: input.subject.scopeOut,
    materiality: input.subject.materiality,
    materialityReasonCodes: input.subject.materialityReasonCodes,
    repositoryBindingIdentity: input.subject.repositoryBindingIdentity,
    pathRoot: input.subject.pathRoot,
    effects: input.subject.effects,
    impactSignals: input.subject.impactSignals,
    lpsId: input.subject.lpsId,
    lpsVersion: input.subject.lpsVersion,
    doctrineDigest: input.subject.doctrineDigest,
    optionRefs: input.subject.optionRefs,
    presentedAtIso: input.subject.presentedAtIso,
  });
  if (selfCheck.presentationDigest !== input.subject.presentationDigest) {
    return fail(
      "HD_SUBJECT_DIGEST_MISMATCH",
      "Sceau de présentation incohérent — enregistrement refusé.",
    );
  }

  // CR-02 — Product-qualified re-seal; forged subject with recomputed digest fails.
  const projectOk = await input.oa.projectServices.getProject.execute({
    projectId: input.subject.projectId,
  });
  if (!projectOk.ok) {
    return fail("PRODUCT_PROJECT_REQUIRED", "Project introuvable pour Record.");
  }
  const fresh = await sealGenericMissionForPresentation({
    oa: input.oa,
    projectId: input.subject.projectId,
    cycleInstanceId: input.subject.cycleInstanceId,
    analysis: input.analysis,
    missionSubjectId: input.subject.missionSubjectId,
  });
  if (!fresh.ok) {
    return fail(fresh.code, fresh.message);
  }
  const presentedDigest = (
    input.presentedPresentationDigest ?? input.subject.presentationDigest
  ).trim();
  const status =
    selected === GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_ACCEPT_PREPARE
      ? ("accepted" as const)
      : selected === GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REFUSE
        ? ("refused" as const)
        : ("amended" as const);

  // M-HD-02 — identical replay (incl. after legitimate LPS link bump) returns durable state.
  const history = await input.oa.decisionServices.listDecisionHistory.execute({
    projectId: input.subject.projectId,
    subject: input.subject.missionSubjectId,
  });
  if (!history.ok) {
    return fail(
      "OA_DECISION_HISTORY_UNREADABLE",
      "Historique OA illisible — Record refusé.",
    );
  }

  const lpsNow = await input.oa.projectServices.getCurrentLivingProjectState.execute({
    projectId: input.subject.projectId,
  });
  const digestDiverged =
    fresh.subject.presentationDigest !== presentedDigest ||
    fresh.subject.presentationDigest !== input.subject.presentationDigest.trim();
  // Fail-closed when digest diverged and LPS cannot prove a linked bump.
  if (!lpsNow.ok && digestDiverged) {
    return fail(
      "LPS_CURRENT_UNREADABLE",
      "État Living Project courant illisible — rejeu / Record refusé.",
    );
  }
  const currentLps: LpsMaterialSnapshot | null = lpsNow.ok
    ? lpsMaterialSnapshotFromLivingProjectState(lpsNow.livingProjectState)
    : null;

  const findAlreadyApplied = (
    decisions: readonly HumanDecision[],
    lps: LpsMaterialSnapshot | null,
  ): HumanDecision | null => {
    for (const prior of decisions) {
      const assessed = assessApplicableGenericMissionDecision({
        decision: prior,
        subject: fresh.subject,
        currentLps: lps,
      });
      if (!assessed.applicable) continue;
      if (
        assessed.decision.selectedOptionId === selected &&
        assessed.decision.status === status
      ) {
        return assessed.decision;
      }
    }
    return null;
  };

  const already = findAlreadyApplied(history.decisions, currentLps);
  if (already) {
    const priorDigest =
      already.decisionBasis?.genericMissionContext?.presentationDigest?.trim() ??
      "";
    // Replay only when the Pilote retries the SAME presented digest.
    // A newly sealed digest (intentional re-presentation / supersede) must not
    // be collapsed into ALREADY_APPLIED merely because LPS still links the prior HD.
    if (priorDigest && priorDigest === presentedDigest) {
      return {
        ok: true,
        decision: already,
        humanDecisionCreated: false,
        executionAttemptCreated: false,
        code: "GENERIC_MISSION_HUMAN_DECISION_ALREADY_APPLIED",
      };
    }
  }

  // No same-presentation replay — digest divergence means stale presentation.
  if (digestDiverged) {
    return fail(
      "HD_SUBJECT_STALE",
      "Sujet présenté divergent des faits Product courants — nouvelle présentation requise.",
    );
  }

  // Gate A — session actor when provided; LOCAL_PILOTE only via forceLocalAuthority (tests).
  const sessionActor = input.authenticatedPilote?.actor;
  if (!sessionActor?.actorId && input.forceLocalAuthority !== true) {
    return fail(
      "AUTHENTICATED_PILOTE_REQUIRED",
      "Session Pilote vérifiée requise pour enregistrer une HumanDecision de mission.",
    );
  }
  if (
    sessionActor?.actorId &&
    input.authenticatedPilote?.githubUserId &&
    !sessionActor.actorId.includes(input.authenticatedPilote.githubUserId)
  ) {
    return fail(
      "PILOTE_ACTOR_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
      "Acteur OA incohérent avec l'identité de session — enregistrement refusé.",
    );
  }
  const decisionActor: OaActorReference =
    sessionActor ?? LOCAL_PILOTE_ACTOR;

  const scope = `generic-mission:${input.subject.missionSubjectId}`;
  const authority = registerLocalPiloteAuthority({
    authorityResolver: input.oa.authorityResolver,
    scope,
    issuedAt: input.oa.clock.nowIso(),
    actor: decisionActor,
    forceEnable: input.forceLocalAuthority === true,
  });
  if (!authority.ok) {
    return fail(authority.code, authority.message);
  }

  const decisionBasis = decisionBasisFromGenericMissionSubject({
    subject: input.subject,
    selectedOptionRef: selected,
  });

  const decisionId = `dec:gms:${randomBytes(8).toString("hex")}`;
  const linkLps =
    status === "accepted" && input.linkToLivingProjectState !== false;

  const recorded = await input.oa.decisionServices.recordHumanDecision.execute({
    decisionId,
    projectId: input.subject.projectId,
    ...(input.subject.cycleInstanceId
      ? { cycleInstanceId: input.subject.cycleInstanceId }
      : {}),
    subject: input.subject.missionSubjectId,
    options: GENERIC_MISSION_PRESENTED_OPTIONS.map((o) => ({
      optionId: o.optionId,
      label: o.label,
    })),
    selectedOptionId: selected,
    actor: decisionActor,
    authority: "pilot",
    status,
    reversible: true,
    scope,
    rationale: input.rationale,
    authorityEvidenceId: authority.evidenceId,
    decisionBasis,
    supersedeExistingAccepted: input.supersedeExistingAccepted !== false,
    linkToLivingProjectState: linkLps,
    ...(linkLps ? { expectedLpsVersion: input.subject.lpsVersion } : {}),
  });

  if (!recorded.ok) {
    // Concurrent Record: OA LPS_VERSION_CONFLICT is fail-closed (no orphan HD).
    // Re-read durable history — if the winner already applied the same option,
    // return ALREADY_APPLIED (idempotent retry). Otherwise surface the conflict.
    if (recorded.error.detailCode === "LPS_VERSION_CONFLICT") {
      const hist2 = await input.oa.decisionServices.listDecisionHistory.execute({
        projectId: input.subject.projectId,
        subject: input.subject.missionSubjectId,
      });
      const lps2 =
        await input.oa.projectServices.getCurrentLivingProjectState.execute({
          projectId: input.subject.projectId,
        });
      if (hist2.ok && lps2.ok) {
        const winner = findAlreadyApplied(
          hist2.decisions,
          lpsMaterialSnapshotFromLivingProjectState(lps2.livingProjectState),
        );
        const winnerDigest =
          winner?.decisionBasis?.genericMissionContext?.presentationDigest?.trim() ??
          "";
        if (winner && winnerDigest && winnerDigest === presentedDigest) {
          return {
            ok: true,
            decision: winner,
            humanDecisionCreated: false,
            executionAttemptCreated: false,
            code: "GENERIC_MISSION_HUMAN_DECISION_ALREADY_APPLIED",
          };
        }
      }
    }
    return fail(
      recorded.error.detailCode,
      recorded.error.message ?? "RecordHumanDecision refusé.",
    );
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    decision: recorded.decision,
    humanDecisionCreated: true,
    executionAttemptCreated: false,
    code: "GENERIC_MISSION_HUMAN_DECISION_RECORDED",
  };
}

export {
  GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_ACCEPT_PREPARE,
  GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REFUSE,
  GENERIC_MISSION_OPTION_REQUEST_AMEND,
};
```

```
/**
 * MATERIALITY-BLOCK-01 — bind Generic PREPARE documentary missions to the
 * existing Product artifact target routing (pathRoot + cycle workspace).
 *
 * Reuses enrichExecutionIntentFromBinding / resolveArtifactTargetUnderCycleWorkspace.
 * Does NOT invent Git identity, widen pathRoot, authorize, or execute.
 * Nora path/filename proposals remain NON-AUTHORITATIVE candidates (D-PC-09).
 */

import { enrichExecutionIntentFromBinding } from "../f2/activeCycleGovernedContinuation";
import type { IntentAnalysisDto } from "../f2/types";
import type { ExecutionIntentPayload } from "../f2/executionIntentSchema";
import {
  extractArtifactFileNameCandidate,
  isSafeArtifactFileNameLeaf,
  resolveNonAuthoritativeDocumentaryArtifactLeafCandidate,
} from "@/lib/oa/project/domain/artifactTargetRouting";
import type { RuntimeOaStack } from "@/lib/vertical-slice-runtime";
import { collectConcreteMissionPaths } from "./resolveAuthoritativeProductMateriality";

/**
 * Bare safe Markdown leaf (ex. note-de-cadrage.md) is a Nora filename candidate,
 * not an authoritative repo-relative WHERE. Directory paths and traversal
 * (../x.md) keep the already_concrete / fail-closed path.
 */
export function isBareSafeMarkdownLeafPath(raw: string): boolean {
  return isSafeArtifactFileNameLeaf(raw.trim());
}

/**
 * True when every collected "concrete" path is only a bare .md leaf.
 * Mixed sets (leaf + scopeIn directory, or any path with `/`) are NOT leaf-only.
 */
export function concretePathsAreOnlyBareMarkdownLeaves(
  paths: readonly string[],
): boolean {
  if (paths.length === 0) return false;
  return paths.every((p) => isBareSafeMarkdownLeafPath(p));
}

export type GenericPrepareDocumentaryTargetResolution =
  | {
      readonly kind: "not_documentary";
      readonly analysis: IntentAnalysisDto;
      readonly resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null;
      readonly documentaryTargetResolution: "not_applicable";
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: "already_concrete";
      readonly analysis: IntentAnalysisDto;
      readonly resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: string | null;
      readonly documentaryTargetResolution:
        | "not_applicable"
        | "unresolved_nora_candidate";
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: "resolved";
      readonly analysis: IntentAnalysisDto;
      readonly resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: string;
      readonly cycleWorkspaceRoot: string | null;
      readonly artifactFileName: string;
      readonly documentaryTargetResolution: "resolved_product";
      readonly proposedTargetPath: string | null;
      readonly proposedArtifactFileName: string | null;
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: "needs_clarification";
      readonly analysis: IntentAnalysisDto;
      readonly code: string;
      readonly message: string;
      readonly documentaryTargetResolution: "unresolved_needs_clarification";
      readonly resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null;
    }
  | {
      readonly kind: "blocked";
      readonly analysis: IntentAnalysisDto;
      readonly code: string;
      readonly message: string;
      readonly documentaryTargetResolution: "blocked";
      readonly resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null;
    };

function normalizeHaystack(
  parts: readonly (string | null | undefined)[],
): string {
  return parts
    .filter((s): s is string => typeof s === "string" && s.trim().length > 0)
    .join("\n")
    .normalize("NFC")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[àáâäã]/g, "a")
    .replace(/[èéêë]/g, "e")
    .replace(/[ìíîï]/g, "i")
    .replace(/[òóôöõ]/g, "o")
    .replace(/[ùúûü]/g, "u")
    .replace(/ç/g, "c");
}

/**
 * Documentary eligibility for Generic PREPARE destination composition.
 * Broader than ACGC Artifact-continuation verbs (includes « réaliser ») and
 * includes expectedOutcome/scope — without reclassifying code-like missions.
 */
export function isGenericPrepareDocumentaryMission(
  analysis: IntentAnalysisDto,
): boolean {
  if (!analysis.parseOk) return false;
  if (analysis.executionIntent?.intentKind === "docs_write") return true;

  const hay = normalizeHaystack([
    analysis.objective,
    analysis.rephrasedRequest,
    analysis.expectedOutcome,
    analysis.scope,
    analysis.executionIntent?.artifactBrief,
    analysis.executionIntent?.targetPath,
    ...(analysis.executionIntent?.contentRequirements ?? []),
  ]);
  if (!hay.trim()) return false;
  if (
    /\b(parlons|parler|qu'est[- ]ce|explique|expliquer|explique-moi)\b/.test(
      hay,
    ) ||
    /\?\s*$/.test(hay.trim())
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  // Code / refactor / applicative config stay non-documentary even with a repo
  // frame. Pending-offer rebuild may set intentKind "other" while text remains
  // documentary — do not treat intentKind=other as an automatic non-doc gate.
  const hasExplicitDocumentaryCue =
    /\b(document|note|markdown|\.md\b|specification|cahier|memoire)\b/.test(
      hay,
    ) ||
    (/\blivrable\b/.test(hay) &&
      /\b(documentaire|cadrage|reference|markdown)\b/.test(hay));
  if (
    /\b(refactor|refactore|module|fonction|classe|api|endpoint|service|compil|typescript|javascript|\.ts\b|\.tsx\b|\.js\b|\.jsx\b|\.json\b|\.ya?ml\b|configuration|config(?:uration)?|source|code|applicatif|tsconfig|package\.json)\b/.test(
      hay,
    ) &&
    !hasExplicitDocumentaryCue
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const productionVerb =
    /\bmaterialis/.test(hay) ||
    /\b(realiser|produire|produis|ecrire|rediger|deposer|creer|prepare|preparer|consolider|capitaliser)\b/.test(
      hay,
    );
  // "fichier" alone is insufficient (avoids TypeScript/config false positives);
  // require a documentary cue, or fichier + markdown/note/document/cadrage.
  const documentaryObject =
    hasExplicitDocumentaryCue ||
    (/\bfichier\b/.test(hay) &&
      /\b(markdown|\.md\b|document|note|cadrage)\b/.test(hay));
  const repoOrDeliverableFrame =
    /\b(depot|repo|repository|git|workspace|projet|cycle|emplacement|reference)\b/.test(
      hay,
    ) || Boolean(analysis.executionIntent?.targetPath?.trim());

  return productionVerb && documentaryObject && repoOrDeliverableFrame;
}

function leafFromAnalysis(analysis: IntentAnalysisDto): string | null {
  const ei = analysis.executionIntent;
  return extractArtifactFileNameCandidate({
    artifactFileName: ei?.artifactFileName,
    targetPath: ei?.targetPath,
  });
}

/**
 * When documentary WHERE is absent, compose a server-owned destination under
 * Project.pathRoot + active cycle repositoryWorkspaceSegment before materiality.
 */
export async function resolveGenericPrepareDocumentaryTarget(input: {
  readonly oa: RuntimeOaStack;
  readonly projectId: string;
  readonly cycleInstanceId?: string | null;
  readonly analysis: IntentAnalysisDto;
}): Promise<GenericPrepareDocumentaryTargetResolution> {
  const analysis = input.analysis;
  if (!isGenericPrepareDocumentaryMission(analysis)) {
    return {
      kind: "not_documentary",
      analysis,
      resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null,
      documentaryTargetResolution: "not_applicable",
    };
  }

  const existingPaths = collectConcreteMissionPaths(analysis);
  // Bare safe Markdown leaf alone (note-de-cadrage.md) is NOT authoritative WHERE
  // — fall through to Product cycle-workspace composition. Paths with `/`
  // (including ../secret.md) keep already_concrete / fail-closed; never salvage
  // basename from a forbidden path. scopeIn directories stay concrete.
  if (
    existingPaths.length > 0 &&
    !concretePathsAreOnlyBareMarkdownLeaves(existingPaths)
  ) {
    return {
      kind: "already_concrete",
      analysis,
      resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null,
      documentaryTargetResolution: analysis.executionIntent?.targetPath?.trim()
        ? "unresolved_nora_candidate"
        : "not_applicable",
    };
  }

  const projectResult = await input.oa.projectServices.getProject.execute({
    projectId: input.projectId,
  });
  if (!projectResult.ok) {
    return {
      kind: "blocked",
      analysis,
      code: "PRODUCT_PROJECT_UNAVAILABLE",
      message:
        "Projet Product indisponible — destination documentaire non résoluble (fail-closed).",
      documentaryTargetResolution: "blocked",
      resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null,
    };
  }

  const binding = projectResult.project.repositoryBinding ?? null;
  if (!binding?.identity?.trim() || !binding.pathRoot?.trim()) {
    return {
      kind: "blocked",
      analysis,
      code: "REPOSITORY_BINDING_UNAVAILABLE",
      message:
        "Repository binding Product absent ou incomplet — destination documentaire non résoluble (fail-closed).",
      documentaryTargetResolution: "blocked",
      resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null,
    };
  }

  const requestedCycleId = input.cycleInstanceId?.trim() || null;
  let activeCycleTypeId: string | null = null;
  if (!requestedCycleId) {
    // Pathless documentary under automatic workspaces requires an active cycle
    // segment (01-cadrage, …). Binding checked first; then cycle.
    return {
      kind: "needs_clarification",
      analysis,
      code: "DOCUMENTARY_TARGET_CYCLE_REQUIRED",
      message:
        "Mission documentaire sans chemin concret et sans cycle actif — précisez un fichier Markdown sûr ou activez le cycle de workspace avant préparation.",
      documentaryTargetResolution: "unresolved_needs_clarification",
      resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null,
    };
  }

  const cycleLoad = await input.oa.cycleServices.getCycle.execute({
    cycleInstanceId: requestedCycleId,
  });
  if (!cycleLoad.ok || cycleLoad.cycle.projectId !== input.projectId) {
    return {
      kind: "blocked",
      analysis,
      code: "ACTIVE_CYCLE_INCOHERENT",
      message:
        "Cycle demandé incohérent avec le projet — destination documentaire non résoluble (fail-closed).",
      documentaryTargetResolution: "blocked",
      resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null,
    };
  }
  if (cycleLoad.cycle.status !== "active") {
    return {
      kind: "blocked",
      analysis,
      code: "ACTIVE_CYCLE_NOT_ACTIVE",
      message:
        "Cycle référencé non actif — destination documentaire non résoluble (fail-closed).",
      documentaryTargetResolution: "blocked",
      resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null,
    };
  }
  activeCycleTypeId = cycleLoad.cycle.cycleTypeId;

  const proposedTargetPath =
    analysis.executionIntent?.targetPath?.trim() || null;
  const proposedArtifactFileName =
    analysis.executionIntent?.artifactFileName?.trim() || null;

  const leaf =
    leafFromAnalysis(analysis) ||
    resolveNonAuthoritativeDocumentaryArtifactLeafCandidate({
      textFields: [
        analysis.objective,
        analysis.rephrasedRequest,
        analysis.expectedOutcome,
        analysis.scope,
        analysis.executionIntent?.artifactBrief,
      ],
      cycleTypeId: activeCycleTypeId,
    });

  if (!leaf && !proposedTargetPath) {
    return {
      kind: "needs_clarification",
      analysis,
      code: "DOCUMENTARY_TARGET_FILENAME_REQUIRED",
      message:
        "Mission documentaire sans fichier Markdown déterminable — indiquez un nom de fichier sûr (ex. note-de-cadrage.md) ; le serveur composera le chemin sous le workspace Project et le cycle actif.",
      documentaryTargetResolution: "unresolved_needs_clarification",
      resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null,
    };
  }

  const seedIntent: ExecutionIntentPayload = {
    ...(analysis.executionIntent ?? {}),
    intentKind: "docs_write",
    artifactFileName: leaf ?? proposedArtifactFileName,
    targetPath: proposedTargetPath,
    // Candidate capability only — envelope still resolved at PREPARE.
    requiredCapabilities:
      analysis.executionIntent?.requiredCapabilities?.length
        ? analysis.executionIntent.requiredCapabilities
        : ["cap:cursor.docs_write"],
  };

  const enriched = enrichExecutionIntentFromBinding({
    analysisIntent: seedIntent,
    binding,
    activeCycleTypeId,
  });

  if (enriched.needsTargetClarification || !enriched.executionIntent?.targetPath) {
    return {
      kind: "needs_clarification",
      analysis: {
        ...analysis,
        executionIntent: enriched.executionIntent,
      },
      code: "DOCUMENTARY_TARGET_UNRESOLVED",
      message:
        "Destination documentaire non résoluble sous pathRoot/cycle workspace — chemin invalide, hors périmètre, ou clarification Pilote requise (fail-closed, aucun rewrite silencieux).",
      documentaryTargetResolution: "unresolved_needs_clarification",
      resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: null,
    };
  }

  const resolvedPath = enriched.executionIntent.targetPath.trim();
  const resolvedLeaf =
    enriched.executionIntent.artifactFileName?.trim() || leaf || "";

  return {
    kind: "resolved",
    analysis: {
      ...analysis,
      executionIntent: enriched.executionIntent,
    },
    resolvedAuthoritativeTargetPath: resolvedPath,
    cycleWorkspaceRoot:
      enriched.executionIntent.scopeIn?.[0]?.trim() || null,
    artifactFileName: resolvedLeaf,
    documentaryTargetResolution: "resolved_product",
    proposedTargetPath,
    proposedArtifactFileName,
  };
}
```

```diff
+type StampedSelectInspectionBinding =
+  | {
+      readonly ok: true;
+      readonly expectedVersion: number;
+      readonly expectedSemanticFingerprint: string;
+      /** How the binding was obtained — never an authorization act. */
+      readonly provenance: "caller_explicit" | "durable_attestation";
+    }
+  | { readonly ok: false; readonly code: string; readonly message: string };
+
+const INSPECTION_FINGERPRINT_PATTERN = /^(?:sha256:)?[a-f0-9]{64}$/i;
+
+function isExplicitBindingPresent(value: unknown): boolean {
+  return value !== undefined && value !== null;
+}
+
+function validateExplicitInspectionVersion(
+  value: unknown,
+):
+  | { readonly ok: true; readonly version: number }
+  | { readonly ok: false; readonly code: string; readonly message: string } {
+  if (typeof value !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(value)) {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      code: "BINDING_INVALID",
+      message:
+        "Version d'inspection explicite invalide (NaN / non-finie / type hostile) — Select refusé.",
+    };
+  }
+  if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 1) {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      code: "BINDING_INVALID",
+      message:
+        "Version d'inspection explicite hors domaine — Select refusé.",
+    };
+  }
+  return { ok: true, version: value };
+}
+
+function validateExplicitInspectionFingerprint(
+  value: unknown,
+):
+  | { readonly ok: true; readonly fingerprint: string }
+  | { readonly ok: false; readonly code: string; readonly message: string } {
+  if (typeof value !== "string") {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      code: "BINDING_INVALID",
+      message:
+        "Fingerprint d'inspection explicite invalide (type hostile) — Select refusé.",
+    };
+  }
+  const fp = value.trim();
+  if (!fp || !INSPECTION_FINGERPRINT_PATTERN.test(fp)) {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      code: "BINDING_INVALID",
+      message:
+        "Fingerprint d'inspection explicite vide, whitespace ou malformé — Select refusé.",
+    };
+  }
+  return { ok: true, fingerprint: fp };
+}
+
+/**
+ * Resolve G-D4 inspection binding for stamped Generic Select.
+ *
+ * I1 — both truly absent → durable attestation if sufficient/current.
+ * I2 — both valid present → keep caller values (never overwrite).
+ * I3/I4 — version XOR fingerprint (valid or not) → BINDING_INCOMPLETE.
+ * I5–I9 — explicit invalid / hostile → BINDING_INVALID (never treated as absent).
+ * I11 — missing/stale attestation when fallback needed → refuse.
+ * I12 — never records inspection / HD / Attempt.
+ */
+async function resolveStampedSelectInspectionBinding(input: {
+  readonly oa: RuntimeOaStack;
+  readonly executionContractId: string;
+  readonly expectedVersion?: number;
+  readonly expectedSemanticFingerprint?: string;
+}): Promise<StampedSelectInspectionBinding> {
+  const versionProvided = isExplicitBindingPresent(input.expectedVersion);
+  const fingerprintProvided = isExplicitBindingPresent(
+    input.expectedSemanticFingerprint,
+  );
+
+  if (versionProvided !== fingerprintProvided) {
+    // Presence XOR — validate the provided side for invalid-vs-incomplete clarity,
+    // but always refuse incomplete pairs (I3/I4) without attestation overwrite.
+    if (versionProvided) {
+      const v = validateExplicitInspectionVersion(input.expectedVersion);
+      if (!v.ok) return v;
+    }
+    if (fingerprintProvided) {
+      const f = validateExplicitInspectionFingerprint(
+        input.expectedSemanticFingerprint,
+      );
+      if (!f.ok) return f;
+    }
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      code: "BINDING_INCOMPLETE",
+      message: versionProvided
+        ? "Fingerprint d'inspection requis avec la version explicite — binding partiel refusé."
+        : "Version d'inspection requise avec le fingerprint explicite — binding partiel refusé.",
+    };
+  }
+
+  if (versionProvided && fingerprintProvided) {
+    const v = validateExplicitInspectionVersion(input.expectedVersion);
+    if (!v.ok) return v;
+    const f = validateExplicitInspectionFingerprint(
+      input.expectedSemanticFingerprint,
+    );
+    if (!f.ok) return f;
+    // I2 — preserve explicit valid pair. Do not replace from attestation.
+    return {
+      ok: true,
+      expectedVersion: v.version,
+      expectedSemanticFingerprint: f.fingerprint,
+      provenance: "caller_explicit",
+    };
+  }
+
+  // I1 — both truly absent: server-owned durable attestation only.
+  const inspection = await readContractInspectionState({
+    oa: input.oa,
+    executionContractId: input.executionContractId,
+  });
+  if (!inspection.ok) {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      code: inspection.code,
+      message: inspection.message,
+    };
+  }
+  if (inspection.reinspectionRequired) {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      code: "INSPECTION_STALE",
+      message:
+        "Attestation d'inspection obsolète ou réinspection requise — Select refusé.",
+    };
+  }
+  if (!inspection.inspectionSufficient || inspection.attestedVersion == null) {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      code: "INSPECTION_REQUIRED",
+      message:
+        "Inspection Pilote suffisante requise avant Select (contrat matérialité stampé).",
+    };
+  }
+  const attestedFp =
+    inspection.attestedSemanticFingerprint?.trim() ||
+    inspection.semanticFingerprint?.trim();
+  if (!attestedFp) {
+    return {
+      ok: false,
+      code: "EXPECTED_FINGERPRINT_REQUIRED",
+      message:
+        "Empreinte sémantique d'inspection absente — Select refusé (fail-closed).",
+    };
+  }
+  return {
+    ok: true,
+    expectedVersion: inspection.attestedVersion,
+    expectedSemanticFingerprint: attestedFp,
+    provenance: "durable_attestation",
+  };
+}
```

### Qualification des 7 FAIL `npm test` (env Studio nettoyé)

Comparaison parallèle sur worktree **clean main** (mêmes 6 suites, même env nettoyé):

| Échec | Sur clean main? | Classification |
|---|---|---|
| `importBoundaries` (+4 W2 modules M-HD) | **NON** (PASS main) | **Intégration mécanique requise** — allowlist |
| `productionRuntimeReference.conformance` digests | **NON** (PASS main) | **Intégration mécanique requise** — refresh digests |
| `mw6… B12 product path` | **OUI** | ENVIRONMENT — host `OPENAI_API_KEY` présent; test exige `undefined` (valeur **non reproduite** ici) |
| `f3.fixtureVerticalSlice` timeout 5s | **OUI** | ENVIRONMENT / flaky local (aussi sur main) |
| `qa-pre-m6-01.finalHardening` H1/H2 timeouts | **OUI** | ENVIRONMENT / flaky local (aussi sur main) |
